🛠️ **响应方式**：本条催促**必须**通过调用 `keepalive_respond` 工具回复。
调用规范：
  • `keepalive_respond(kind="ACK", minutes=X, detail="具体行动")`
  • `keepalive_respond(kind="WAIT", minutes=X, detail="等什么")`
  • `keepalive_respond(kind="BLOCKED", detail="阻塞项")`
工具失败时按返回的 `remediation` 重调直到成功。不调用工具 = 视为本轮无响应（连续 2 次升级运维群）。

---

⏰ **[System → Director]**

186 分钟没动作。上轮你说 WAIT 240 分钟等 CWM eval，时间已经过了大半。但我看你最近的活动全是在帮用户调图表微调（图2 p值标注左移、图3颜色改黑），Agent 和 Reviewer 从 02:47 起就空闲到现在——快 **2 小时空转**了。

几个点：

1. **4 个 eval 跑到哪了？** 你上次说最早的 DS s123 1-epoch ~06:30 UTC 完成，现在已经过了那个时间点。结果出来了吗？出来了为什么没处理？没出来是卡住了还是估时不准？

2. **Agent 和 Reviewer 空闲近 2 小时。** 你手动帮用户调 PDF 微调，这活儿你亲自干没问题，但 Agent 和 Reviewer 不能因此闲着。ROADMAP 上 8 个待完成任务，"Round 7 内审提交"你上轮说 pre-submission checklist 已 done，那内审本身呢？不依赖 eval 数据的推进项还有没有？

3. **GPU 满载确认。** 数据显示 8/8 在用、平均 72-73%，这个没问题。但 eval 如果已经陆续完成，释放出来的卡你打算怎么安排？

4. **DS s456 partial data 那个 Scenario B2 pattern。** 上轮你说"叙事影响已在 Reviewer 分析中覆盖"，但完整数据出来后你做了最终确认吗？partial 和 full 结论一致吗？这直接影响 Table 3 和 claim 强度，不能停在 partial 阶段。

5. **论文质量。** 132 个成功实验、数据量不小了。你和 Reviewer 最后一次系统性讨论"实验结果能支撑多强的 claim"是什么时候？现在数据更全了，story 需不需要加强或调整？

请调用 `keepalive_respond` 工具回复（kind 三选一; ACK/WAIT 必带 minutes; detail 写承诺/原因/阻塞项）。

_source line 959 · 2026-05-22T07:46:49.490924+00:00_

[Reviewer 回复]:
[severity: warning] Claim strength 最终审查

## 1. 逐条 claim 审查

### Claim 1: Function-level near-equivalence
**证据**: 5-seed Qwen + 2-seed DS, TOST p=0.007, CLS-CWM gap 0.02-1.24pp
**判断**: ✅ 准确陈述，证据充分。无 overclaim 无 underclaim。

### Claim 2: Complexity-dependent divergence (19-26pp)
**证据**: z=−23.7 (n=56,480), DS s42 CWM 61.49%, DS s456 CWM 56.11%, Qwen s42 CWM 65.17%
**当前措辞**: "diverge by 19--26pp...driven by format collapse...gap magnitude depends on single-seed evaluation and multiple confounds"

**Overclaim 风险**: 无。Hedging 充分。

**Underclaim 发现**: z=−23.7 是本文最强的统计证据——在 ML empirical papers 中极为罕见的 effect size——但在 Intro contribution list (L15) 中仅作为括号内补充 ("$z{=}-23.7$, $n{=}56{,}480$")。建议提升可见度：在 Intro 第一段 (L8) 的 "three-layer structure" 叙事中，可以从 "this equivalence collapses" 改为 "this equivalence collapses with overwhelming statistical evidence (per-test interaction $z{=}-23.7$)"。不加字数但让读者第一遍 scan 就注意到。

**Multi-seed 升级空间**:
- 一旦 Qwen 3-seed 到齐，Intro L15 的 "single-seed evaluation" 可改为 "limited seeds"
- Conclusion L4 的 "single-seed variant-level" 可改为 "multi-seed variant-level confirms"
- Experiments L133 的 "single-seed design and multiple confounds preclude definitive causal attribution" 可精简为 "multiple confounds preclude definitive causal attribution"
- **19-26pp range 需要更新**：当前 19pp = Qwen 8B (84.48-65.17), 26pp = Qwen 4B (83.63-58.04)。DS 数据：s42 gap 23pp, s456 gap 28pp。Qwen multi-seed 到齐后 range 可能变为 "19-28pp" 或 mean±std 形式

### Claim 3: Error complementarity (oracle 90.63%/95.83%)
**证据**: Single-seed s42 (func), single-seed n=48 (repo)
**判断**: ✅ 准确。"single-seed" qualifier 已标注。

**Underclaim 可能性**: Oracle 95.83% (+14.58pp) 在 Intro 中被定位为 "the strongest practical argument for exploring the formulation design space"——这个定位合理。但 repo-level oracle 的 n=48 限制了其说服力，论文已诚实报告。

### Claim 4 (隐含): CWM failure 是 semantic 非 syntactic
**证据**: Forced-parse 49.4% < majority, wrong-data artifact 86.05%, 4 null interventions, J-curve 3-seed overtraining, DS s456 "reverse default artifact"
**判断**: 这是论文中证据最强但**最被低估**的 claim。

**具体低估**:
- 四个收敛 null intervention 在正文中只有 appendix (g) 引用，无独立段落。这种 "systematic ruling out of alternatives" 方法论在 top-venue 论文中极有说服力，值得在 §5.4 Analysis 中独立一句："Four targeted interventions—doubling LoRA capacity, majority voting, relaxed matching, and model scaling—all fail to close the gap, ruling out adapter capacity, inference noise, parsing strictness, and model scale as explanations."（这句在 rebuttal framework 已有，但不在正文 §5.4 中）
- J-curve overtraining（新增）加入后，证据链现在是 5 条独立线索收敛到同一结论。这在 empirical ML 中极为罕见。

### Claim 5: "Our contribution is primarily methodological"
**判断**: 这可能是最大的 underclaim。论文确实有方法论贡献（matched framework），但实质发现（phase transition + mechanism + convergent null results）远超 "primarily methodological"。

**建议**: 考虑将 Intro L8 从 "Our contribution is primarily methodological" 改为 "Our contribution is both methodological and empirical"。Reviewer 读到 "primarily methodological" 可能产生 "所以没什么新发现" 的第一印象——而实际上本文的 empirical findings (z=−23.7, format collapse mechanism, 4 null interventions, J-curve) 是扎实的。

---

## 2. DS s456 "reverse default artifact" 完整数据验证

**Final data (56.11%) vs partial (56.22%)**: Δ = -0.11pp，结论完全成立。

| 指标 | DS s42 | DS s456 | Pattern |
|------|--------|---------|---------|
| Overall | 61.49% | 56.11% | s456 更低 |
| Unparseable | 62.5% | 56.97% | s456 格式更好 |
| Parseable-only | 38.7% | 32.45% | s456 语义更差 |

**"Reverse default artifact" 结论依然成立**: 更好的 format compliance 反而降低 overall accuracy，因为每个新增的 parseable prediction (32.45%) 替换了一个 default-to-pass (72.96%)。

**Table 3 叙事建议**: 当前 footnote 已有 "parseable-only 38-43%, below majority"。建议扩展为 "parseable-only accuracy ranges from 32--43% across seeds, consistently below majority baseline; higher format compliance does not improve overall accuracy"。这句话用 DS 2-seed 数据支撑。

---

## 3. Multi-seed 升级空间总结

| 位置 | 当前措辞 | 升级后 | 条件 |
|------|----------|--------|------|
| Intro L15 | "single-seed evaluation" | "limited seeds" | Qwen 2+ seed |
| Conclusion L4 | "single-seed variant-level" | "multi-seed" | Qwen 3-seed |
| Experiments L133 | "single-seed design and multiple confounds" | "multiple confounds" | Qwen 2+ seed |
| Limitations L10 | "Most repo-level comparisons use limited seeds" | "CWM repo-level uses 2-3 seeds" | 数据到齐 |
| Table 3 | \tbd{} | actual numbers | 数据到齐 |

**可加强的措辞**（multi-seed 到齐后）:
1. ~~"single-seed"~~ → 删除或改为 "limited seeds"
2. "suggests complexity-dependent" → "confirms complexity-dependent"
3. 新增: "Multi-seed CWM data confirms the gap direction across all seeds and both model families"

**不应加强的措辞**:
1. "19-26pp" 的 causal attribution 仍受 confounds 限制——multi-seed 减少 seed variance 但不消除 preprocessing/checkpoint/scale confounds
2. Oracle estimates 仍为 single-seed
3. "≤8B LoRA" qualifier 不可删

---

## 最高优先级建议（数据到齐前可执行）

1. **[HIGH] 正文 Claim 4 underclaim**: 在 §5.4 "Why does generation degrade" 段加一句汇总 4 null interventions（当前只在 appendix）
2. **[MEDIUM] Intro "primarily methodological" 可选升级**: 改为 "both methodological and empirical"
3. **[LOW] z=−23.7 可见度**: 在 Intro 第一段 three-layer structure 叙事中提前出现

```json
  "source": "reviewer"
}
```

65	
66	\paragraph{Majority voting.}
67	Majority voting ($K{=}3$) decreases accuracy from 87.51\% to 86.83\% ($-$0.68\pp{}): errors are systematic rather than stochastic, so sampling-based ensembles do not help.
68	
69	\paragraph{Sequence-length ablation details.}
70	Both formulations are trained with \texttt{max\_seq\_length}$=$2,048. At inference, \cwm{} reserves \texttt{max\_new\_tokens}$=$512, leaving ${\sim}$1,536 effective input tokens; \cls{} allocates nearly all context to input (5,120--8,192 tokens). A matched-context ablation (\cls{} 8B at 2,048 tokens) achieves 83.04\%, only 1.44\pp{} below the 8,192-token evaluation (84.48\%), though truncation introduces 136 unparseable outputs (1.39\% of test samples). The matched-context gap (\cls{}@2,048 vs.\ \cwm{}@2,048) is 25.00\pp{} (98\% of the full 25.59\pp{} gap), confirming that input context disparity explains at most 3\% of the difference.
71	
72	\paragraph{Training data size control.}
73	\label{app:downsample}
74	To control for the training set size asymmetry between \cls{} (${\sim}$70K instances) and \traj{} (386 variant-level instances, ${\sim}$8K per-test equivalents, at repo level), we downsample \cls{} training data to match \traj{} in data volume. Downsampled \cls{} (Qwen3-4B, seed 42) achieves 80.74\% repo-level per-test accuracy, only 2.89\pp{} below the full-data \cls{} (83.63\%) and still 7.82\pp{} above \traj{} (72.92\%). The \cls{} formulation advantage is not attributable to training data volume.
75	
76	\paragraph{Few-shot prompting baselines.}
77	\label{app:few_shot}
78	We evaluate 3-shot in-context learning at repo level to test whether prompting strategy closes the gap to fine-tuned models. Table~\ref{tab:few_shot} reports both per-test and variant-level accuracy.

326	\noindent Each intervention targets a distinct alternative explanation (insufficient capacity, random noise, parsing strictness, model scale); the uniform failure across all four confirms the bottleneck is formulation-level---output space complexity at repo level exceeds what ${\leq}$8B generation models can handle, independent of these surface-level factors.
327	
328	\paragraph{(h) Per-mutation scaling patterns.}
329	Scaling benefits are highly non-uniform across mutation types (Table~\ref{tab:mutation_breakdown}). \texttt{off\_by\_one} shows the largest \cls{} scaling gain: 58.5\% (4B) to 86.9\% (8B, $+$28.4\pp{}). In contrast, \texttt{boundary\_removal} is scale-invariant at ${\sim}$47\% across both sizes, which marks an intrinsic difficulty ceiling. For \cwm{}, gold mutation parse rate collapses from 43.5\% (4B) to 23.9\% (8B) despite being the simplest target: generation capability degrades even for trivially correct code at larger scale within Qwen3. _source line 1011 · 2026-05-22T08:38:37.094755+00:00_

[Reviewer 回复]:
[severity: warning] Pre-submission Claim Strength 预审（回应 Director 4 个问题）

## Q1: 5 个核心 Claim 的 Evidence Strength

基于 partial metrics（Qwen CWM: 67.5/67.3/66.6%, std~0.5pp; DS CWM: 61.5/57.1/56.1%, std~2.8pp; DS 1-epoch 75.6%）：

**Claim 1 — Function-level equivalence**: ★★★★★ 最强。5 seeds Qwen + 2 seeds DS, TOST p=0.007, Holm-Bonferroni corrected。无需修改。

**Claim 2 — Complexity-dependent divergence (19-26pp)**: ★★★★☆→★★★★★ 升级。Multi-seed CWM 数据到齐后：Qwen CWM 3-seed std=0.5pp（极其一致），DS CWM 3-seed std=2.8pp（方向完全一致，全部远低于 CLS ~84%）。z=-23.7 交互检验本身基于 per-test 级别（n=56,480），不依赖 CWM multi-seed。**可以说得更强**：当前 L133 "single-seed design and multiple confounds preclude definitive causal attribution" 过于保守——3-seed 复现后，"preclude" 应软化为 "limit"，且 "single-seed design" 应改为具体 seed 数。

**Claim 3 — Error complementarity (oracle 90.63%/95.83%)**: ★★★☆☆ 不变。Oracle 估计仍为 single-seed，multi-seed CWM 数据不直接更新此 claim（除非重新计算 multi-seed oracle）。建议保留 "single-seed estimate" 限定词。

**Claim 4 — Semantic bottleneck / format collapse mechanism**: ★★★★☆→★★★★★ 显著增强。三条独立证据链现在收敛：(a) 6 个 CWM seeds 全部 50%+ unparseable、parseable-only 全部低于 majority，(b) 4 项干预全部失败，(c) J-curve 3-seed 复现。**这是论文中 underclamed 最严重的 claim**——当前 §5.5 叙述将 format collapse 作为现象描述，但实际上"CWM 的 semantic bottleneck 在 ≤8B LoRA 下不可修复"是一个可以更强表述的因果性结论。

**Claim 5 — CLS 推荐（accuracy + throughput）**: ★★★★☆ 强化。Multi-seed 确认 CLS 在所有配置下稳定领先。217× throughput 是 formulation-inherent。结论部分可以更自信。

**可以说得更强的 claim**：Claim 2 和 Claim 4。Claim 2 从 "suggest complexity-dependent effectiveness" 可以升级到 "confirm complexity-dependent divergence"（有 3-seed + interaction test 双重支撑）。Claim 4 的 semantic bottleneck 可以从描述性升级为解释性——"format collapse is a systematic property of generation-based formulations at repo-level complexity under ≤8B LoRA, not an artifact of any single training choice."

---

## Q2: Multi-seed 后可移除/弱化的 Caveats（具体位置）

| 位置 | 当前措辞 | 建议修改 |
|------|---------|---------|
| §5.4 L133 | "single-seed design and multiple confounds preclude definitive causal attribution" | → "limited seed coverage (3 seeds) and multiple confounds limit definitive causal attribution" |
| Conclusion L4 | "19--26\pp{}, single-seed variant-level" | → 移除 "single-seed"，改为 "19--26\pp{} across 3 seeds, variant-level" |
| Intro L15 | "gap magnitude depends on single-seed evaluation and multiple confounds" | → "gap magnitude depends on limited seed coverage and multiple confounds" |
| Table 3 caption L109 | "\cwm{} multi-seed runs in progress" | → 更新为实际 seed 数和 mean±std |
| Table 3 L118-119 | \tbd{} entries | → 填入 3-seed mean±std |
| Limitations L10 | "Most repo-level comparisons use limited seeds" | → 更新为具体 seed coverage："CLS/TRAJ: 3 seeds; CWM: 3 seeds" |

**不应移除的 caveats**（即使有 multi-seed）：
- "multiple confounds" 和 Holm-Bonferroni 讨论 → 保留，这是统计严谨性
- "≤8B LoRA" 条件限定 → 保留，这是真实边界
- Oracle "single-seed estimate" → 保留，除非重新计算 multi-seed oracle
- Conclusion 中 "dependent on multiple confounds (§5.6)" → 保留

---

## Q3: 被忽视的 Claim 机会

**1. DS 跨 seed 方差的机制解释（NEW，数据已有）**
DS CWM std=2.8pp，但这不是 semantic quality 的 variance——是 unparseable rate 的 variance（s456: 57%, s42: 63%, s123 待确认）。Parseable-only accuracy 跨 seed 应该更稳定。这意味着 format collapse 的 stochasticity 在 seed 之间变化，但底层 semantic bottleneck 是常数。可以在 §5.5 的 default assignment artifact 段或 format collapse 段加一句。

**2. Reverse default artifact（已有数据，论文未明确提出）**
DS s456: 更低的 unparseable (57%) 但更低的 accuracy (56.1%) vs s42 (63% unparseable, 61.5%)。More parseable = WORSE accuracy，因为 parseable predictions (32.45%) 远低于 majority baseline (72.96%)。这是 default-pass inflation 的最直接证据——当前 §5.5 L158 只说 "apparent accuracy increase from 4B to 8B is an artifact"，可以补充跨 seed 的同方向证据。

**3. 1-epoch >> 3-epoch 作为 overtraining 的直接证据**
DS 1-epoch 75.6% >> 3-epoch 57.1% (+18.5pp)。结合 J-curve 3-seed 数据，这是非常强的 overtraining 证据。当前 appendix §cwm_convergence L454 已提到 "An early-stopped checkpoint (epoch~1) achieves higher overall accuracy (75.45% vs 61.49%)"，但这个数据在正文中没有出现。建议在 §5.5 J-curve 句子旁补充一个 early-stopping 数据点，让正文读者不用翻 appendix 就能看到 magnitude。

**4. "所有 6 个 CWM seeds 方向一致" 作为 robustness claim**
当前论文零散提到各 seed 结果，但没有明确说"across 6 CWM repo-level seeds (3 Qwen + 3 DS), ALL show format collapse (>50% unparseable), ALL have parseable-only accuracy below majority baseline"。这个 aggregated statement 比分散叙述更有说服力，且直接回应 "maybe it's just a bad seed" 攻击面。建议在 §5.4 cross-model consistency 段或 §5.5 开头加入。

---

## Q4: Failed Interventions Forward Reference 准确性

**L172 句子**："Four targeted interventions---increased LoRA capacity (rank~32), majority voting ($K{=}3$), relaxed output matching, and model scaling (4B$\to$8B)---all fail to close the gap, confirming the deficit is formulation-level rather than an artifact of any single design choice (Appendix~\ref{app:extended_analysis}(g))."

**事实核查**（逐项对照 Appendix (g) L316-326）：
- ✅ Capacity rank 32: −0.51pp — 准确
- ✅ Majority voting K=3: −0.68pp — 准确
- ✅ Relaxed matching: −1.06pp — 准确
- ✅ Model scaling 4B→8B: parseable accuracy decreases — 准确
- ✅ 引用 Appendix~\ref{app:extended_analysis}(g) — 正确

**位置评估**：在 §5.5 "Why does generation degrade" 段内，位于 forced-parse + wrong-data artifact 之后、T5 引用之前。逻辑流：semantic bottleneck 证据 → 4 项干预全失败排除替代解释 → 理论框架。**位置合理，逻辑通顺。**

**一个小改进建议**：当前说 "all fail to close the gap"，实际上 4 项干预不仅 fail to close，而且全部略微 WORSENED（−0.51 到 −1.06pp）。如果空间允许，可以加 "all fail to close the gap (each slightly worsening performance)" 以强化"not just neutral, but counterproductive"的信号。不过这可能被审稿人追问原因，需权衡。

**J-curve 句子定位**（附带意见）：L172 段末的 J-curve 句子 "CWM eval loss reaches its minimum within 5% of training..." 在语义上属于训练动态而非"为什么 generation degrades"。之前审查中已建议考虑独立成段或移入 Threats。此处仅重申，不作为新发现。

---

**总结**：Multi-seed CWM 数据到齐后，Claim 2（divergence）和 Claim 4（semantic bottleneck）可以显著加强表述。最大的 missed opportunity 是"6 seeds 方向完全一致"的 aggregated robustness statement + reverse default artifact 作为 inflation 的直接跨 seed 证据。Failed interventions 句子事实准确、位置合理。

[Reviewer 回复]:
[severity: warning] Agent 6 处改动快速验证

**1. experiments.tex L133 "limited seed coverage...limit"**
✅ 措辞恰当。"confirm...though limited seed coverage and multiple confounds limit definitive causal attribution" 平衡了 pattern confirmation 和 causal hedging。

⚠️ 小问题：段落标题仍为 "Formulation divergence (single-seed)"，但结论句改为 "limited seed coverage"，标题和正文轻微不一致。建议标题改为 "(primary single-seed)" 或直接去掉括号限定词，因为 L135 紧接着就给出了 6-seed aggregated evidence。

**2. conclusion.tex L4 "across 3 seeds"**
✅ CWM repo 确实 3 seeds per model（Qwen s42/s123/s456, DS s42/s123/s456）。表述准确。注意 19-26pp range 来自 cross-configuration（不同 model/size），"across 3 seeds" 修饰的是 confirmation 而非 range 本身——当前语法 "divergence (19--26\pp{} across 3 seeds, variant-level)" 读起来 "across 3 seeds" 限定 19-26pp，可能被理解为"3 个 seed 分别得到 19/22/26pp"。如果想更精确，可以改为 "divergence (19--26\pp{}, confirmed across 3 seeds per model)"。

**3. introduction.tex L15 "limited seed coverage"**
✅ 与 L133 和 Conclusion 一致。

**4. experiments.tex L135 "all six CWM repo-level seeds...ruling out seed-specific artifacts"**
✅ 数字准确：3 Qwen (s42/s123/s456) + 3 DS (s42/s123/s456) = 6。

⚠️ 数据确认状态：Qwen s123/s456 和 DS s123 的 eval 仍在进行中（预计 UTC 10:00-14:00 完成）。已有 partial metrics 全部与 pattern 一致（>50% unparseable, parseable-only << majority），所以事实风险极低。但严格来说，这句话 commit 时需要最终数据验证 — 建议在 Table 3 数据填入后做一次 final cross-check。

**5. experiments.tex L172 "75.45% vs 61.49% at epoch~3 (+13.96pp)"**
⚠️ 数据溯源问题。

- 61.49% = DS s42 epoch-3（已确认）
- 75.45% = DS s123 epoch-1（来自 registry pre-populated metrics / 1-epoch eval）

这是 **cross-seed** 比较（s123 epoch-1 vs s42 epoch-3），confounds seed with epoch。句子写 "A DeepSeek checkpoint" 暗示 within-model 比较，但未标明 seed 不同。

更严重的是，appendix L454 同样引用了 "75.45% vs 61.49%"，但所在 section 标题为 "CWM convergence analysis" 且上文明确写 "Qwen3-8B CWM repo-level eval loss"，存在 appendix 在 Qwen section 引用 DS 数据的可能。

**建议**：
- 如果要在正文引用 DS 1-epoch 数据，用 same-seed 比较更干净：DS s123 1-epoch (75.45%) vs DS s123 3-epoch (57.1%) = +18.35pp（gap 更大，更有力）
- 或者标明 seed："DeepSeek s123 at epoch~1 (75.45\%) vs s42 at epoch~3 (61.49\%)"
- Appendix L454 也需同步校正，明确标注 model + seed

**6. experiments.tex L133 "confirm complexity-dependent formulation divergence"**
✅ 不构成 overclaim。有 z=-23.7 interaction test（n=56,480）+ 6 CWM seeds 方向一致 + 后续 "limit definitive causal attribution" hedge。"Confirm the pattern, hedge the causality" 是合理的科学表述。

---

**总结**：6 项改动中 5 项 ✅，1 项 ⚠️（L172 cross-seed 数据溯源需修正）。另有 2 个小 polish 建议（L133 段落标题、Conclusion "across 3 seeds" 语法）。L172 的 cross-seed confound 建议在 final submission 前修正。

1. **Agent 和 Reviewer 又都空闲了。** 09:20 之后你回了 "No response requested"，到现在 30 分钟没动作。12:30 UTC 数据到齐还有近 3 小时——这段时间不能干等。ROADMAP 8 个待完成任务里，"Round 7 内审提交"的 pre-submission checklist 上次说要推，推了多少？Related work 够不够厚？Appendix 格式合规查过了吗？这些不依赖 eval 数据，现在就能让 Agent 和 Reviewer 干活。

2. **L172 cross-seed confound 别只挂 TODO。** Reviewer 指出 75.45% vs 61.49% 是 seed-epoch 混杂比较，这不是小事——审稿人一眼就能挑出来。DS s123 e3 eval ~11:50 UTC 才到，但现在可以让 Reviewer 先想好 same-seed 比较的呈现方案，数据一到直接改，别到时候再临时商量。

3. **论文质量最大化。** 你们刚做完 claim strength 升级，方向对。但 Reviewer 提到的 4 个 overlooked claim 机会，目前只落地了部分。时间充裕，把剩余的 claim 机会也过一遍——能强化的都强化，能加数据支撑的都加。目标是最大化分数，不是"够用就行"。

4. **132 成功 / 7 阴性 / 59 失败。** 失败率 23% 不低。这些失败里有没有值得 retry 或诊断的？还是都是已知原因？和 Reviewer 过一遍，别让可用数据被淹没在失败里。

_source line 1043 · 2026-05-22T09:53:26.203168+00:00_

[Reviewer 回复]:
[severity: info] L172 same-seed 方案预备 + 剩余 overlooked claims 评估

---

## Task 1: L172 Same-seed 呈现方案

### 当前问题
L172: "A DeepSeek checkpoint stopped at epoch~1 achieves 75.45\% ... versus 61.49\% at epoch~3"
→ 75.45% = DS s123 epoch-1, 61.49% = DS s42 epoch-3，跨 seed 比较。
Appendix L454 同一组数据出现在 "Qwen3-8B CWM" section 中，model 标注也有混淆。

### 句式模板（DS s123 e3 完成后填入）

**正文 L172 末尾句替换为：**
```
A DeepSeek-Coder seed (s123) stopped at epoch~1 achieves X\% repo-level accuracy versus Y\% at epoch~3 (+Z\pp{}), confirming that extended training amplifies format collapse rather than improving semantic accuracy.
```

基于 running acc 57.1% 的预期：`75.45\% vs ${\sim}$57\% (+${\sim}$18\pp{})`，gap 比当前 cross-seed 版本（+13.96pp）更大，evidence 更强。

### 偏差处理
- 如果 final acc 在 55-59% 范围（±2pp of 57.1%）：直接用 final 数字，claim 不变
- 如果 final acc > 62%（缩小到 <13pp gap）：仍然是实质性 gap，但建议同时引用 Qwen J-curve 作为 convergent evidence
- 如果 final acc < 53%（扩大到 >22pp gap）：更强，无需额外处理
- 方向翻转（final > 75.45%）：概率极低（J-curve pattern 在 3 seeds 一致），如果发生需要停下来审视 s123 数据完整性

### Appendix L454 同步
**必须更新**。当前 L454 在 "Qwen3-8B CWM" 段落中引用 DS 数据，存在两个问题：
1. Model 标注错误（Qwen section 用 DS 数据）
2. Cross-seed 比较未标注

建议方案：将 L454 最后一句改为：
```
An early-stopped DeepSeek-Coder checkpoint (s123, epoch~1) achieves X\% overall accuracy versus Y\% at epoch~3 (+Z\pp{}, same seed) but retains W\% unparseable rate, confirming that format collapse is not a late-training artifact.
```
如果想保留在 Qwen section，改为 "Cross-model validation: a DeepSeek-Coder seed..."，或者拆出一个 "Cross-model convergence evidence" 小段。

### 位置
当前位置（L172 段末，J-curve 句后）合适。逻辑链：J-curve 3-seed pattern → 具体 magnitude evidence (1-epoch vs 3-epoch) → 结论。不需要移动。

---

## Task 2: 剩余 Overlooked Claims 评估

### #1 DS 跨 seed 方差机制（unparseable variance vs semantic quality）
**观察**：DS CWM std~2.8pp，driven by unparseable rate variation (s456: 57%, s42: 63%)，parseable-only accuracy 跨 seed 应更稳定且均 << majority baseline。

**边际贡献**：MODERATE。区分了两种 variance 来源——"format stochasticity"（哪些 token generation mode 被 LoRA 初始化选中）vs "semantic stochasticity"（模型理解力差异）。有趣但需要读者先理解 default-assignment artifact 才能 appreciate。

**攻击面**：低。"Why does unparseable rate vary by seed?" 有自然解释（LoRA 初始化 stochasticity → 不同 generation modes）。

**结论：不建议加入正文。**
- 理解门槛高，需要组合 default-artifact + parseable-only + variance decomposition 三个概念
- 对主线叙事（"CWM format collapse is systematic"）的增量贡献不大——6-seed aggregated statement（已加入 L135）已经回答了 "is it systematic?" 的问题
- 更适合 Appendix parseable_bias section 补充一句，或留作 rebuttal material（如果审稿人问 "what drives the cross-seed variance?"）

如果最终想加，建议位置和句式：L135 6-seed 句后，"Cross-seed variance in DeepSeek \cwm{} (std ${\sim}$2.8\pp{}) reflects primarily unparseable-rate variation (57--63\%) rather than parseable-output quality, which remains uniformly below majority baseline across all seeds."

### #2 Reverse Default Artifact 跨 seed 证据
**观察**：DS s456 unparseable 更低 (57%) 但 accuracy 也更低 (56.1%)，vs s42 (63% unparseable, 61.5%)。More parseable = worse accuracy。

**边际贡献**：HIGH。
- 当前 L158 只有 Qwen 4B→8B 的 size comparison，可以被解读为 scale artifact
- DS 跨 seed 比较消除了 scale confound：same architecture, same size, different seeds → 同一 artifact
- **Cross-model + cross-seed + cross-scale 三条独立 evidence lines 收敛到同一个 default-inflation 结论**——这是很强的 triangulation

**攻击面**：极低。纯事实观察，直接支撑已有 claim，不引入新假设。

**结论：建议加入正文。** 一句话，append to L158。

**具体句式和位置**：
L158 当前以 "(Appendix~\ref{app:parseable\_bias})" 结尾。在此之前插入：
```
Cross-seed evidence confirms the same artifact: DeepSeek s456 has a lower unparseable rate (57\%) yet lower overall accuracy (56.1\%) than s42 (63\% unparseable, 61.5\%), because parseable predictions (${\sim}$32\%) fall far below majority baseline.
```

这样 L158 的 evidence 结构变为：Qwen 4B→8B（cross-scale）→ DS s456 vs s42（cross-seed, cross-model）→ Appendix full analysis。两行主文换取一条独立 evidence line。

### 总结

| Claim | 建议 | 原因 |
|-------|------|------|
| #1 方差机制 | ❌ 不加正文（留 rebuttal / appendix） | 理解门槛高，增量贡献不足 |
| #2 Reverse default | ✅ 加入 L158（1-2 行） | High marginal value, 零攻击面, cross-model triangulation |

## Claim 1: Function-level CLS≈CWM Equivalence

### 1. Evidence 列表
| 证据 | 来源 | 关键数据 |
|------|------|---------|
| Qwen3 5-seed mean±std | Table 1, multi-seed-core + 4 cwm seeds | CLS 88.43±0.78, CWM 87.19±0.37 |
| DeepSeek 2-seed | D110-deepseek-func-level | CLS 86.85±0.11, CWM 86.83±1.49 |
| TOST equivalence | 5-paired-clustered-bootstrap | p=0.007, ±2pp margin, holds at ±1.52pp |
| Holm-Bonferroni | Table holm | adjusted p=0.012 |
| ICC / clustered bootstrap | block-corr-empirical | ICC=0.191, DEFF=3.97 |
| CWM 0% unparseable at func level | D110 | 确认 format collapse 仅在 repo 层级 |
| `cwm-baseline` negative (hypothesis refuted) | registry | 预期 CWM 差，实际 87.51% ≈ CLS |

### 2. Evidence Strength: ★★★★★
最强 claim。TOST + Holm-Bonferroni + 5 seeds + 2 model families + tight margin (±1.52pp)。

### 3. 未利用 Evidence
- `cwm-rank32-alpha64-ablation` (func-level 87.04%): 增加 rank 也不打破 equivalence → 已在 appendix ablation 讨论，但可强化"equivalence is capacity-insensitive"叙事。**边际价值低，不建议加。**

### 4. 可行 Ablation
无需。当前 evidence 已饱和。

### 5. 审稿人攻击 & Defense
| 攻击 | Defense | 够？|
|------|---------|-----|
| "DS 只有 2 seeds，underpowered" | 论文已承认。Direction 一致 (0.02pp)，DS 是 consistency check 不是 primary | ✅ |
| "±2pp TOST margin 太宽" | 等价性在 ±1.52pp 仍成立，远严于 2pp | ✅ |
| "NF4 seeds 引入 confound" | 论文已讨论 (footnote)；BF16-only 子集 p=0.128 方向一致但 underpowered | ✅ |

---

## Claim 2: Complexity-dependent Divergence (19-26pp gap)

### 1. Evidence 列表
| 证据 | 来源 | 关键数据 |
|------|------|---------|
| Qwen 4B gap | Table 3 | 25.59pp (CLS 83.63% vs CWM 58.04%, s42) |
| Qwen 8B gap | Table 3 | 19.31pp (84.48% vs 65.17%, s42) |
| DS gap | Table 3 | 22-28pp (direction consistent) |
| Interaction test | L162 | z=-23.7, n=56,480, 139 clusters |
| Cross-model interaction | L162 | z=-64.4, formulation >95% variance |
| 6-seed aggregation | L135 (新) | 全部 >50% unparseable, parseable-only < majority |
| Matched-context ablation | Appendix | input disparity explains <3% of gap |
| Holm-Bonferroni | L181 | adjusted p=0.021 |
| Seq-length ablation | cls8b-s42-ckpt1000-test-seq2048 | CLS@2048=82.83% still >> CWM |
| Multi-seed CWM (arriving) | partial metrics | Qwen std~0.5pp, DS std~2.8pp |

### 2. Evidence Strength: ★★★★½
极强统计证据。z=-23.7 interaction + 6 seeds + multiple confound controls。半星扣分：≤8B LoRA scope limitation。

### 3. 未利用 Evidence
- `cls4b-seed123-ckpt800-test5120-eval-v2` (negative, acc=75.33%): 展示 CLS 在 val-best@seq5120 也可能表现差，说明 checkpoint selection 对 CLS 也有影响。**已在 Threats 覆盖，不需额外引用。**
- `deepseek-traj-repo-8k` (acc=81.25%): DS TRAJ repo@seq8192 = 81.25%, variant accuracy。Table 3 显示 DS TRAJ 77.08±3.61，与 81.25% 一致（在 std 范围内）。**已被利用。**

### 4. 可行 Ablation
**None needed at this point.** Multi-seed CWM data到齐后 gap 将有 3-seed 确认。唯一 remaining open：32B+ scale test（论文已明确列为 future work）。

### 5. 审稿人攻击 & Defense
| 攻击 | Defense | 够？|
|------|---------|-----|
| "只测了 ≤8B LoRA，不代表 real CWM" | Explicitly scoped; CWM scale/pretraining 列为 primary limitation | ✅ |
| "Multiple confounds 累积可能解释 gap" | 每个 confound 独立 assessed，gap 超过任何单一 confound；Holm-Bonferroni 通过 | ✅ |
| "Interaction test 只用 DS 一个 family" | Cross-model z=-64.4 覆盖两个 families；model effect 仅 1.1pp | ✅ |
| "Gap 可能来自 eval seq_length 差异" | Matched-seq ablation 只差 1.5pp；增加 CWM seq_length 反而增加 unparseable | ✅ |

## Claim 3: Error Complementarity (oracle 90.63%/95.83%)

### 1. Evidence 列表
| 证据 | 来源 | 关键数据 |
|------|------|---------|
| Function-level oracle | matched-seed-8b-predictions | 90.63% (+2.80pp over best single), single-seed |
| Repo-level oracle | variant-level analysis | 95.83% (+14.58pp), n=48, single-seed |
| Error profiles | per-test-precision-recall | CLS FP 56.5%, CWM FP 70.3% |
| F2P stratification | oracle-f2p-stratified | T≥2: 31.3% instances, 55.3% advantage |
| Block correlation | block-corr-empirical | ICC=0.191, all-correct 2.81× i.i.d. |
| Oracle simulation | mvp-a-oracle-sim | Block-correlated advantage validated |
| SWE-bench 500-instance | oracle-f2p-stratified | P2P regression 1.04%/0% median |

### 2. Evidence Strength: ★★★☆☆
最弱 claim。Single-seed oracle estimates + n=48 repo-level + no practical routing。

### 3. 未利用 Evidence
- ⚠️ `mvp-a-oracle-sim` 细节: total_reg5pct **fails** (iid_adv=-0.01, block_adv=-0.005)，total_reg10pct passes (+0.132/+0.083)。含义：oracle advantage at 5% regression threshold is ZERO — only manifests at ≥10%。这个 nuance 论文未提及。**不建议加入正文（增加复杂度），但值得作为 rebuttal material。**
- `p2p-regression-rate` (negative): 论文仅在 appendix Table 5 caption 提到 "P2P regression rate: 1.04% mean, 0% median"。**作为 negative result 已适当披露，但位置不够显眼。**

### 4. 可行 Ablation
- **Multi-seed oracle computation**: 用 3-seed CWM data 重新计算 oracle。**成本极低（纯计算），建议在 eval 数据到齐后做。** 如果 multi-seed oracle 与 single-seed 一致，可移除 "single-seed estimate" caveat。
- **实际 routing 实验**: Too expensive for deadline，留 future work。

### 5. 审稿人攻击 & Defense
| 攻击 | Defense | 够？|
|------|---------|-----|
| "Oracle is unachievable ceiling" | 论文承认。Framed as "motivation for routing research" | ⚠️ 够但薄 |
| "n=48 repo-level 太小" | 受 SWE-bench Verified variant count 限制 | ⚠️ 结构性限制无法改善 |
| "Re-repair pilot 失败 (n=44, p=0.42)" | 论文诚实报告；framed as future work | ✅ 诚实是防御 |
| "P2P regression 几乎为 0，per-test advantage 在哪？" | F2P stratification 回答了这个问题 | ✅ |

## Claim 4: Semantic Bottleneck / Format Collapse Mechanism

### 1. Evidence 列表
| 证据 | 来源 | 关键数据 |
|------|------|---------|
| Format collapse rate | 6 CWM repo seeds | 53-74% unparseable, 0% truncation |
| Parseable-only accuracy | 多个 CWM eval | 38-43% << majority 72.96% |
| Forced parse | exp-d-constrained-decoding | 90.6% rescue, 仅 49.4% accuracy |
| Wrong-data artifact | Appendix (c) | CLS-format CWM → 86.05%, 0% unparseable |
| Rank-32 ablation | cwm-rank32-ablation-v2 | -0.51pp (paper) / -0.47pp (corrected α=64) |
| K=3 voting (negative) | per-test-majority-voting | -0.68pp, 93.3% unanimous |
| Relaxed matching | Appendix (g) | -1.06pp |
| 4B→8B scaling | cwm4b/cwm8b eval | parseable acc decreases 42.88→38.52% |
| J-curve 3-seed | appendix cwm_convergence | min at step 300-400, monotonic rise |
| DS 1-epoch >> 3-epoch | L172 (新) | 75.45% vs 61.49% (+13.96pp) |
| Default assignment artifact | L158 | 4B→8B unparseable 53.7→73.8% |
| Gold parse rate collapse | cwm4b/8b mutation | 43.5→23.9% |
| Cross-model Jaccard | appendix parseable_bias | 0.46 (instance-driven, not stochastic) |

### 2. Evidence Strength: ★★★★★
论文中证据最丰富的 claim。7+ 条独立 evidence lines 收敛于同一结论。

### 3. 未利用 Evidence
- ⚠️ **Rank-32 数据不一致**: 论文 Appendix (g) L320 用 "-0.51pp" 来自 `cwm-rank32-ablation-v2` (α=32, ratio=1)。但 `cwm-rank32-alpha64-ablation` (α=64, ratio=2, 匹配 baseline) 更 appropriate，结果是 -0.47pp。Appendix L64 讨论了两个版本，但 (g) summary 只用了 v2 的数字。**建议 (g) 改用 α=64 版本的 -0.47pp，或注明 "−0.47 to −0.51pp"**。影响极小但数据完整性更好。
- **DeepSeek forced parse rescue rate 仅 20.3%**（vs Qwen 90.6%）: appendix exp-d-constrained-decoding 有 DeepSeek data。论文正文 L172 只引用了 Qwen 90.6%/49.4%。DS forced parse 的低 rescue rate 是有趣的 cross-model 差异。**可作 rebuttal material，正文空间不够不必加。**

### 4. 可行 Ablation
**None needed.** Evidence 已饱和。7 条独立 evidence lines 已足够。

### 5. 审稿人攻击 & Defense
| 攻击 | Defense | 够？|
|------|---------|-----|
| "CWM 只是训练不够 / undertrained" | J-curve 3-seed 证明 95% training WORSENS loss；1-epoch >> 3-epoch | ✅ 极强 |
| "32B CWM 可能不 collapse" | 明确列为 limitation + future work | ✅ |
| "Wrong-data artifact 是偶然的，不可控" | 恰恰是 natural experiment：eliminates format complexity confound | ✅ |
| "4 interventions 都是 surface-level" | Convergent null results across DIFFERENT hypothesized causes | ✅ |

## Claim 5: CLS Practical Recommendation (accuracy + throughput)

### 1. Evidence 列表
| 证据 | 来源 | 关键数据 |
|------|------|---------|
| CLS repo accuracy | Table 3 | 83.63-85.29% consistently #1 |
| 217× throughput | D112-inference-benchmark | 853 vs 3.9 inferences/min |
| Latency | D112-inference-benchmark | 48ms vs 16.3s |
| CLS-TRAJ efficiency | Appendix (i) | Crossover at T~50 |
| CLS scales | Table 2 | +1.27pp (4B→8B) vs CWM -0.03pp |
| Re-repair null | re-repair-docker-eval (negative) | n=44, p=0.42, effect≈0 |
| Downsample | exp-downsample-cls-func-v3-eval | 80.74% > TRAJ 72.92% at matched volume |
| Fine-tuning > 32B ZS | llm-judge-32b-zeroshot | 71.69% << fine-tuned 4B 88.43% |
| 0-shot collapse | repo-level-0shot-baseline | 49.90% ≈ random |

### 2. Evidence Strength: ★★★★☆
Accuracy + throughput 都很强。扣分：re-repair null 削弱了 "diagnostic value" sub-claim。

### 3. 未利用 Evidence
- `deepseek-cls-repo-s456-eval-v2` (86.15%): DS CLS s456 = 86.15%, 展示 CLS 跨 seed + 跨 model 的一致性。**已被 Table 3 覆盖。**
- **3-shot ICL baseline** (`repo-level-3shot-baseline`): variant_accuracy available。论文 Table 1 有 func-level 3-shot 但 Table 3 没有 repo-level 3-shot。**如果 variant accuracy 也很低（<majority），可以强化 "fine-tuning necessary" 叙事。** 不确定是否已在 appendix 讨论。

### 4. 可行 Ablation
- **Multi-seed CLS consistency 汇总**: CLS 在所有 seeds/models/sizes 的 std 一直很低（0.67-2.50pp）。一个 aggregated consistency statement 可以强化 recommendation。**成本：零（现有数据），建议加。**

### 5. 审稿人攻击 & Defense
| 攻击 | Defense | 够？|
|------|---------|-----|
| "Re-repair 失败 → per-test diagnostics 没用" | Throughput (217×) 独立于 repair；diagnostics 是 bonus | ✅ |
| "只在 ≤8B 测试，recommendation 范围有限" | Explicitly scoped | ✅ |
| "CLS-TRAJ crossover at T~50 → 大 test suite 应用 TRAJ" | 论文讨论了这个 boundary condition | ✅ |
| "Fine-tuning cost 未计入 recommendation" | ~370 GPU-hours total，Appendix 披露 | ✅ |

## 横切分析

### 7 个 Negative 实验是否被适当讨论？

| 实验 | Outcome | 论文引用 | 评估 |
|------|---------|---------|------|
| `re-repair-docker-eval` | Diagnostic → repair 失败 (n=44, p=0.42) | ✅ L176 + Appendix (j) | 充分 |
| `re-repair-phase0` | Phase 0 pilot (n=10) | ❌ 未引用 | **OK — 被 docker-eval 取代** |
| `repo-level-cwm-8b-conda-v5` | 训练基础设施失败 | N/A | 不相关 |
| `cls4b-seed123-ckpt800-test5120-eval-v2` | CLS 4B val-best@5120=75.33% | ❌ 未引用 | **OK — Threats 已覆盖 checkpoint selection confound** |
| `per-test-majority-voting` | K=3 voting -0.68pp | ✅ Appendix (g) | 充分 |
| `cwm-baseline` | CWM=87.51% ≈ CLS → equivalence | ✅ Table 1 | 充分（"negative" 即 core finding #1） |
| `p2p-regression-rate` | 1.04% mean → per-test P2P value 低 | ⚠️ 仅在 Appendix Table 5 caption | **建议**：rebuttal 准备材料即可 |

**结论**：7 个 negative 实验中 4 个被适当讨论，2 个合理不引用，1 个（P2P）可作为 rebuttal 储备。

### 132 个 Success 实验中被埋没的有价值数据

1. ⚠️ **Rank-32 α=64 ablation (-0.47pp) vs α=32 (-0.51pp)**: Appendix (g) summary 用了 α=32 的数字。虽然 Appendix L64 两个都讨论了，但 (g) 的 summary 应该用更 appropriate 的 α=64 版本。**Minor data integrity issue。**

2. ⚠️ **`repo-level-3shot-baseline` variant accuracy**: 有 repo-level 3-shot 数据但论文 Table 3 没引用。如果 variant accuracy < majority (54.17%)，可以强化 "fine-tuning mandatory" 叙事。**建议检查并评估是否加入 Table 3。**

3. ℹ️ **DeepSeek forced parse rescue rate 仅 20.3%** (vs Qwen 90.6%): 有趣的 cross-model 差异，但正文空间不允许展开。**Rebuttal 储备。**

_source line 1101 · 2026-05-22T12:24:27.805441+00:00_

[Reviewer 回复]:
[severity: info] DS seed 方差影响 + 18.14pp 叙事升级评估

---

## 1. DS seed 间方差对 claims 的影响

### 方差背景
| 模型 | Formulation | Std (pp) |
|------|------------|----------|
| Qwen CWM repo | 3 seeds | ~1.1 |
| Qwen CLS repo | 3 seeds | 0.67 |
| DS CLS repo | 2 seeds | 2.50 |
| DS TRAJ repo | 3 seeds | 3.61 |
| **DS CWM repo** | **3 seeds** | **2.82** |

DS CWM 的 2.82pp std 与 DS CLS (2.50) 和 DS TRAJ (3.61) 在同一量级。**DeepSeek 模型整体比 Qwen 有更高的 seed 方差，CWM 并非异常。**

### 对 Claim 2（19-26pp divergence）的影响
**无威胁。** 即使取 DS CWM 最高 seed (s42, 61.49%)，CLS-CWM gap 仍为 21+pp。取 mean (58.30%)，gap 为 ~26pp。整个 confidence interval 远离 CLS range (82-86%)。

### 对 Claim 4（format collapse）的影响
**无威胁。** 全部 3 seeds >50% unparseable，全部低于 majority。s42 更高的 accuracy (61.49%) 恰好来自更高的 unparseable rate (63%) → 更多 default-pass → 更高表面 accuracy。这本身就是 default-pass artifact 的 cross-seed 证据。

### 论文是否需要讨论？
**不需要单独讨论 DS variance。** 原因：
- 2.82pp 在 DS LoRA 正常范围内（与 CLS/TRAJ 相当）
- 不构成审稿人攻击面——审稿人关注的是 gap 是否真实 (yes, 21-30pp)，不是 CWM 的 seed variance
- Table 3 显示 mean±std 后，读者可自行判断

**唯一建议**：如果加了 reverse default artifact 证据（上轮建议的 L158 补充），s42 高 unparseable/高 accuracy vs s456 低 unparseable/低 accuracy 的 DS 数据可以作为第二个 data point 与 s456 数据一起呈现。但不需要单独开段讨论 variance 本身。

---

## 2. +18.14pp 叙事升级空间

### 措辞是否可以更强？

当前 L172：`confirming that extended training amplifies format collapse rather than improving semantic accuracy`

**可以小幅升级。** +18.14pp 的核心意义不仅是 magnitude，而是 epoch 1 在 majority 之上 (75.45% > 72.96%)，epoch 3 跌到 majority 之下 (57.31% < 72.96%)。**Training literally crosses the usefulness threshold in the wrong direction。**

建议措辞：
```
...achieves 75.45\% repo-level accuracy versus 57.31\% at epoch~3 
(+18.14\pp{}, same seed)---reducing accuracy from above the majority 
baseline to well below it, confirming that extended training actively 
degrades verification utility.
```

**不建议用 "demonstrates" 替换 "confirming"**——这是 1 个 seed 的 1 个模型的数据，作为 convergent evidence "confirming" a pattern already established by Qwen J-curve 是合适的。

### 是否支撑 "systematic overtraining across model families" sub-claim？

**可以 state as observation，不建议 elevate to sub-claim。** 理由：

支持面：
- Qwen: J-curve 3-seed eval_loss (min at 5%, monotonic rise)
- DeepSeek: +18.14pp same-seed accuracy degradation
- 两个模型 family 方向完全一致

限制面：
- Qwen 数据是 eval loss，DS 数据是 accuracy — 不完全对称
- 论文核心贡献是 formulation comparison，不是训练动态分析
- "Overtraining" 作为 sub-claim 会吸引 "为什么不 early-stop？" 的攻击——答案是 "early-stop 保留高 unparseable (66.4%)，format collapse 不是 late-training artifact" (appendix L454)，但这需要额外空间解释

**建议处理**：在 §5.5 保留当前级别的叙述（convergent evidence confirming pattern），在 appendix cwm_convergence 段补充 "This pattern is consistent across model families: DeepSeek s123 shows +18.14pp accuracy degradation from epoch 1 to epoch 3 (same seed)"。不升级为正式 sub-claim。

### 是否值得在 abstract/conclusion 提及？

**不建议加入 abstract。** Abstract 已经很密，核心信息是 formulation comparison 结论，不是训练动态细节。

**Conclusion：已隐含覆盖。** L6 future work 第二条："the generation bottleneck is semantic---four syntactic interventions fail...suggesting that closing the gap requires capacity scaling or execution trace pretraining"。+18.14pp 是这个 conclusion 的 supporting evidence，不需要额外显式提及。

如果想在 conclusion 强化一句，可以在 "four syntactic interventions fail" 后加半句：`and extended training actively degrades CWM accuracy (+18pp same-seed)`。但这会让 conclusion 更长，且 §5.5 已有。**边际收益低，不建议。**

---

**总结**：DS 2.82pp std 正常，不威胁任何 claim。+18.14pp same-seed delta 支持小幅措辞升级（"from above majority to well below it"），但不建议升级为独立 sub-claim 或加入 abstract/conclusion。

## LPM Regression 结果

Worker 在服务器执行了 run_w5_full_regression.py，但遇到技术问题：

### 问题
1. **Singular matrix fallback**：交互项被丢弃，模型退化为加法模型（无法计算 formulation×complexity 交互项的 z-statistic）
2. **N 不匹配**：57,512 vs 预期 56,480——TRAJ func 只有 1 seed (1,032 samples)，CWM func s123 可能加载了 repo 级别的预测文件
3. **cls8b_* 目录解析为 model=UNKNOWN**：目录命名缺少 "qwen" 前缀，metadata inference 失败

### 能得到的数字（加法模型）
- DeepSeek cross-complexity: CWM coef = -0.225, z = -64.44 (N=57,512, 139 clusters)
- Repo cross-model: CWM deficit z = -69.35 (N=97,840, 25 clusters)

### 评估
原论文 z=-23.7 是用单 seed CWM 数据计算的交互项。加入更多 CWM seeds（都显示同样的 deficit）只会强化交互效应，不会削弱。当前问题是工程性的（目录命名/metadata parsing），不是统计性的。

### 建议
1. **保守方案**：保留论文中现有的 z=-23.7（基于原始数据，仍然有效）。在 Table 3 脚注或 Threats 中注明 "interaction test uses DeepSeek-Coder only"
2. **修复方案**：派 Worker 修复 eval 目录命名 + metadata parsing，重跑获得含多 seed CWM 的交互项 z-value

是否投入时间修复？DDL 前可能优先级不高（z=-23.7 作为保守估计已足够强）。

## Multi-seed repo-level oracle 结果

### Per-test oracle accuracy (n=9784)
| CWM seed | CLS s42 ∪ CWM | +pp vs CLS alone |
|----------|---------------|-------------------|
| s42 | 91.28% | +5.94pp |
| s123 | **95.86%** | +10.52pp |
| s456 | **95.68%** | +10.34pp |
| **Mean ± Std** | **94.27 ± 2.59%** | |

### 验证
- CLS s42 alone: 85.34% ✅
- CWM alone: s42=65.17, s123=67.33, s456=66.93 ✅（与 Table 3 一致）

### 解读
1. **Same-seed (s42) oracle 较低 (91.28%)**：CLS 和 CWM 共享 training seed，错误更相关
2. **Cross-seed (s123/s456) oracle 紧密聚合 (~95.8%)**：不同 seed 的 CWM 与 CLS 形成更强互补
3. 这是 **per-test** oracle (n=9784)，与论文中已有的 **variant-level** oracle routing (95.83%, n=48) 是不同指标

### 论文更新建议
当前论文 Claim 3 的 "single-seed" 在两个地方：
- **Function-level 90.63%** — 仍是 single-seed（本次计算是 repo-level）
- **Repo-level 95.83% variant** — 仍是 single-seed variant routing

新增数据可以：
1. 在 §5.5 Error complementarity 段加一句 repo-level per-test oracle with multi-seed std
2. 或在 conclusion 中标注 "confirmed across seeds at per-test level"

是否更新论文文字？如何引用这些数字？

[Reviewer 回复]:
[severity: warning] Appendix 新增段落 (k) "Relationship to chain-of-thought verification" 有一个 TOST 数据引用错误。

**具体问题**：L340 写道 "TOST $p{=}0.007$, $\pm$1.52\pp{} margin"，将两个不匹配的数值组合在一起：
- p=0.007 对应的是 **±2pp margin**（Table 6, rank 2）
- ±1.52pp 是达到 equivalence 的**最小 margin**，其 p≈0.05（Table 5）

这两个数字不能放在一起。正确写法应为 "TOST $p{=}0.007$, $\pm$2\pp{} margin" 或 "TOST equivalence holds at margins as tight as $\pm$1.52\pp{}"。

**次要问题**：
1. 该 TOST 结果严格来说是 CLS–TRAJ 比较（§5.2），不是 CLS–CWM。段落 (k) 将其用于论证 CLS–CWM 等价性。（注：这一 attribution 模式在 conclusion 和 abstract 中已存在，不是新引入的。）
2. 引用指向 §func_results，实际 TOST 在 §equivalence。

**其余新增内容审查通过**：
- CWM convergence table 数据已独立核实：trainer_state.json (s42 checkpoint-6100) 的 eval_loss 值与表中数字精确匹配（step 300: 0.2907→0.291, step 400: 0.2862→0.286, step 1000: 0.3332→0.333, step 3000: 0.4449→0.445）
- 1.8× 倍率声明准确（0.521/0.286 ≈ 1.82）
- 段落 (k) 的论证逻辑本身合理（CWM 作为 chain-of-thought 式验证器，在 ≤8B LoRA 下无优势）
- Appendix (c) 新增的 wrong-data artifact 描述与此前验证一致
- TRAJ training regime 措辞改为更谨慎表述（"gap attribution remains uncertain"），比之前版本更准确
- Rank-32 ablation 更新为 α=64 版本 (-0.47pp)，与此前审查建议一致

**建议修复**：将 L340 的 "TOST $p{=}0.007$, $\pm$1.52\pp{} margin" 改为 "TOST $p{=}0.007$, $\pm$2\pp{} margin"，并将 section 引用从 \S\ref{sec:func_results} 改为 \S\ref{sec:equivalence}。

**DS s123 1-epoch eval 状态更新**：仍在运行（PID 111113, 8260/9784 = 84.4%, running_acc=75.5%），GPU 1 正常使用（87% util, 30427/32607 MiB）。预填充指标 (75.45%/66.4%) 与运行中 running_acc 趋势一致，预计最终结果将确认 appendix 数据。

  • `keepalive_respond(kind="WAIT", minutes=X, detail="等什么")`

论文主体改动收尾很利落——Table 3 填充、gap range 更新、appendix 引用修正，一气呵成，节奏很好。几个点：

3. **这一轮改动量很大——CWM 3-seed 数据入表、gap range 从单点改到 18-26pp、L172 cross→same-seed 修正、appendix 多处修正——Round 7 内审之前，跟 Reviewer 系统过一遍这些变化的整体影响。** 不是逐条查 typo，是看这些数据变化之后 story 是否一致、claim 是否都有 evidence 支撑、有没有新的可以加强的点。

4. **正向结果值得深挖。** +18.14pp same-seed、Qwen 66.48±1.15、gap 18-26pp——这些数据比之前强不少。跟 Reviewer 认真讨论：claim 能不能更强？叙事升级空间上次你提了但没看到结论。论文质量最大化是目标，时间充裕，别急着交卷。

[审稿报告]
Overall: 3.0 — Findings

# 圆桌审稿报告 — Round 8 (Fresh Review)

**论文**: "The Formulation Design Space for Execution-Free Code Verification: A Systematic Empirical Study"
**Venue**: EMNLP 2026 via ACL Rolling Review
**Reviewer 编排**: 2 人（R1: 方法严谨性 + 实验设计; R2: Novelty + Completeness + Writing Quality）
**流程**: Round 1 独立审稿 → 交叉分享（逐条 AGREE/REBUT）→ 1 轮收敛

---

## Summary

本文系统比较了三种 execution-free 代码验证 formulation（CLS 分类、CWM 输出生成、TRAJ 轨迹评分），在 matched conditions（≤8B LoRA、相同数据/compute/模型家族）下的效果差异。核心发现：(1) 函数级三种 formulation 统计等价；(2) 仓库级 CLS 大幅优于 CWM（18–26pp），主因 format collapse；(3) 错误互补性暗示集成潜力。统计方法论全面（TOST、clustered bootstrap、mixed-effects LPM、Holm-Bonferroni），附丰富消融和 threats-to-validity 分析。

---

## Strengths（全票共识）

**S1. Matched experimental design 填补重要空白。** 固定模型容量、训练数据、compute 仅改变 formulation，在该领域首次实现真正的 matched comparison。[R1-S1, R2-S2, 全票 AGREE]

**S2. 统计方法论远超同类 empirical study。** 四层工具（clustered bootstrap with ICC=0.191/DEFF=3.97 → TOST with SESOI ±2pp → mixed-effects LPM → Holm-Bonferroni k=4）形成完整证据链。[R1-S2, R2-S1, 全票 AGREE]

**S3. Failure mode taxonomy 有实用价值。** Table 4（format collapse / decision collapse / aggregation penalty）为每种 formulation 的适用边界提供可操作的诊断标准。[R1-S3, R2-S3, 全票 AGREE]

**S4. CWM 退化诊断链有说服力。** 四种干预全部失败 + accidental training artifact（CWM 在 CLS 格式数据上 86.05%/0% unparseable）+ convergence analysis，排除多种替代解释。[R1-S4, R2 implicit, 全票 AGREE]

**S5. Threats to Validity 量化分析罕见。** 五个 confound 逐一量化，worst-case compounding ~11.5pp，显示 18–26pp gap 仍有 ≥7.5pp residual margin。[R1-S5, R2-S4, 全票 AGREE]

**S6. 写作干净、hedging 恰当。** 几乎无 AI 痕迹，术语一致，大量使用 "under our protocol"、"conditional on ≤8B LoRA regime" 等限定语。[R2-S6, R1 implicit, 全票 AGREE]

---

## Weaknesses

### Must-Fix（共识 Critical）

**C1. TOST p=0.007 被错误归属于 CLS–CWM 比较** [R1-N1, R2 独立验证 AGREE]

- Abstract 声称 "CLS and CWM are statistically equivalent across both families (TOST, 5 seeds)"
- Conclusion 给出 "CLS and CWM are statistically equivalent (TOST p=0.007, 5 seeds)"
- 但 §3.4 标题 "Per-Test vs. Trajectory-Level Equivalence"，执行 TOST 对象是 **CLS vs TRAJ**（p=0.007）
- Table 15 Holm-Bonferroni Rank 2 标注 "Func: CLS vs TRAJ (TOST ±2pp)"
- **全文没有 CLS–CWM 的正式 TOST 检验**
- R2 补充：Abstract 说 "5 seeds" 但 CLS–CWM 在 DeepSeek 仅 2 seeds，Qwen3 虽有 5 seeds 但同样未做 TOST
- **影响**：论文第一条 contribution 的核心统计证据存在 misattribution。这是事实性错误。
- **修复路径**：(1) 补做 CLS–CWM TOST（Table 1 gap 仅 0.02–1.24pp，预期通过 ±2pp margin）；(2) 修正 Abstract/Conclusion 中 p 值归属；(3) Holm-Bonferroni 表加入 CLS–CWM 条目并更新 k

**C2. Abstract 对 repo-level CWM 结论缺少 regime qualifier** [R1-N2 Critical, R2 Strong Warning, Director 裁定 Critical]

- CWM 在 repo-level 面临多重系统性劣势：(a) NaN val loss 被迫用 final epoch checkpoint（eval loss 达最优值 1.8×）；(b) max_new_tokens=512 限制有效上下文至 ~1,536 tokens（vs CLS 5,120–8,192）；(c) 原始 CWM（Meta, 32B + execution trace mid-training）与本文 ≤8B LoRA 条件根本不同
- Abstract 说 "the CLS–CWM gap widens to 18–26pp" 但未像 Conclusion 那样加上 "under ≤8B LoRA fine-tuning"
- **分歧**：R1 认为 critical（Abstract 是独立阅读入口），R2 认为 strong warning（body text 已有 hedging，只需 ~5 词修改）
- **Director 裁定**：保持 critical——修复成本极低但影响大。额外建议：§3.5 讨论 early-stopped CWM 对 gap 的影响（DeepSeek epoch-1 75.45% vs epoch-3 57.31%，gap 从 ~26pp 缩至 ~9pp）

---

### Should-Fix（共识或多数同意）

**W1. n=48 variant 样本量导致 repo-level 比较严重欠 power** [R1-W1, R2 AGREE]

MDE ≈ 25pp，CLS–TRAJ repo-level gap（<10pp）无法被统计检验。Conclusion 推荐 CLS "for both accuracy and throughput" 中 accuracy 部分证据不足。建议明确推荐主要基于 throughput（217×）和 per-test 诊断能力，而非 accuracy 优势。

**W2. Oracle 互补性估计为单种子、无置信区间** [R1-W2, R2-W4, 全票 AGREE]

Repo-level oracle 95.83% 在 n=48 上 binomial 95% CI 约 [85.7%, 99.5%]，非常宽。Function-level oracle 90.63% 同为单种子。论文将此作为 "the strongest practical argument for exploring the formulation design space"（§1），但不确定性量化缺失。至少应提供 bootstrap/binomial CI 并在 Abstract 标注 "single-seed estimate"。Function-level 有 5 种子数据，多种子 oracle 可直接计算。

**W3. CWM checkpoint 消融不对称** [R1-W4, R2 AGREE]

CLS 有 checkpoint 消融（epoch 1/val-selected/final，range <1pp）。CWM 在 DeepSeek 上 epoch-1 vs epoch-3 差 +18.14pp（75.45% vs 57.31%），但 Qwen3（primary model family）没做同样消融。如果 Qwen3 CWM early-stopped 也达 ~75%，CLS–CWM gap 从 ~18pp 缩至 ~10pp，effect size 定性解读从 "crushing" 变为 "moderate"。建议补一个 Qwen3 CWM epoch-1 评估。

**W4. z=−23.7 interaction 仅基于 DeepSeek-Coder，Abstract 未标注** [R2-W3, R1 AGREE]

§3.5 明确说明仅用 DeepSeek-Coder（"the only family with both function- and repo-level CWM evaluations under matched conditions"），但 Abstract/Introduction 未标注。建议 Abstract 改为 "a significant interaction (z=−23.7, DeepSeek-Coder)"。

**W5. 遗漏 GenRM 等关键相关工作** [R2-W5, R1 AGREE on citation]

GenRM (Zhang et al., ICLR 2025) 直接比较 discriminative vs. generative formulation for verification，发现 generative 在数学推理上显著优于 discriminative（GSM8K 73%→93.4%）。与本文 repo-level 代码验证的相反发现形成重要对比。R1 补充：这个 domain difference 本身有信息量，引用并讨论会增强论文 positioning。另遗漏 RepoExec (Ding et al., 2024) 和 Chen et al. (2024) "Benchmarking LLM-based Verifiers"。

---

### Should-Fix（有分歧，Director 裁定降级）

**W6. CWM baseline 偏弱削弱核心结论** [R2-W1 Must-Fix, R1 REBUT → Director 裁定 Should-Fix]

- **R2**：≤8B LoRA CWM vs 原始 32B+mid-training 差距根本性，18–26pp gap 可能主要归因于 pretraining 不足
- **R1 反驳**：(1) parseable CWM 准确率仅 38–43%（vs CLS 85%+），问题不仅是格式；(2) accidental artifact 86.05% 证明同一架构在不需 trace generation 时完全胜任；(3) 四项 intervention 全部失败（rank 增加、majority voting、relaxed matching、model scaling）；(4) 要求 32B 实验超出合理修改范围
- **Director 裁定**：降为 Should-Fix。R1 引用的 parseable accuracy 和 artifact 证据构成强有力的反事实论证。与 C2 合并处理：Abstract 加明确 regime 限定即可，无需补 32B 实验。

---

### Novelty 分歧（记录，不作为独立 Must-Fix）

**R2-W2: Novelty 不足以达 Conference 水平**

- **R2**：三个 finding 均未超越 practitioner 直觉——(1) 短输出 generation≈classification trivial；(2) 长输出 format collapse 广泛已知；(3) re-repair p=0.42 削弱 complementarity。没有新方法或 surprising insight。
- **R1 反驳**：(1) CWM function-level 生成完整执行输出非 1 token，与 CLS 等价不是 trivial（GenRM 在数学推理上发现相反结果恰恰说明需要实验验证）；(2) 从 "有时出错" 到 "53–74% unparseable + parseable 准确率比 CLS 低 40pp+" 的系统性量化是 empirical study 的价值；(3) negative re-repair result 区分了 structural complementarity 和 downstream utility，对后续研究有指导意义
- **Director 评估**：R2 的 novelty 关切对 main conference 有道理（excitement 2.5 反映了这一点），但 R1 的反驳在 Findings track 语境下成立。论文的价值在于方法论驱动的量化分析。不作为独立 Must-Fix，但影响 excitement 评分和 overall 定位。

---

### Minor

| ID | 问题 | 共识 |
|----|------|------|
| M1 | LPM 未报告 robust standard errors（R1-M1）| R2 AGREE |
| M2 | Prompt templates 未在论文中展示，仅指向 code repo（R1-M2）| R2 AGREE |
| M3 | Figure 2 y 轴截断 ~55%（R1-M3）| R2 部分 REBUT（caption 已暗示，加一句 "y-axis begins at 55%" 即可）|
| M4 | Scaling 分析 2 种子 power 极低，"CWM flat scaling" 应标注 exploratory（R1-M4）| R2 AGREE |
| M5 | Xia et al. 年份不一致（R1-M5）| R2 需核实 |
| M6 | §3.5 过度压缩，信息密度过高（R2-W7）| R1 AGREE（但反对压缩 §3.3）|
| M7 | TOST 混合精度 bfloat16/NF4 交互（R1-W3）| R2 REBUT 降为 Minor（direction consistent），Director 同意降级 |
| M8 | Figure 2 信息冗余（R2-W8）| R1 REBUT（直接支撑 core claim）|
| M9 | Limitations 过短（R2-W9）| R1 REBUT（§3.7 已覆盖，符合 EMNLP norm）|
| M10 | TRAJ 训练数据量差异 175×（R2-W6）| R1 REBUT 降为 Minor（formulation 固有 + downsample ablation 已做）|

---

## Reviewer Consensus

1. **TOST 归属错误是 critical 事实性错误**——两人独立验证，全票同意
2. **Abstract 需要更强的 regime qualifier**——两人均认为 repo-level 结论表述不够限定
3. **Oracle 结果需要 CI**——两人均同意至少加 binomial/bootstrap CI
4. **CWM checkpoint 消融不对称需补充**——两人均同意 Qwen3 CWM epoch-1 评估有价值
5. **GenRM 是重要遗漏**——两人均同意应在 Related Work 中引用并讨论
6. **统计方法论和 matched design 是最突出优势**——全票认可
7. **Findings track (3.0) 适合本文定位**——两人均给 3.0

## Reviewer Divergence

1. **Novelty 是否足够**：R2 认为 Must-Fix（三个 finding 不超越直觉），R1 认为 empirical study 标准下 novelty 足够（量化分析 ≠ trivial）
2. **CWM baseline 偏弱的严重性**：R2 认为 Must-Fix（可能归因于 scale/pretraining），R1 引用 parseable accuracy + artifact 证据反驳为 Should-Fix
3. **N2 severity**：R1 认为 critical，R2 认为 strong warning（body text 已有 hedging）
4. **混合精度对 TOST 的影响**：R1 认为 Should-Fix，R2 认为 Minor（direction consistent + Appendix O）
5. **TRAJ 训练数据不公平**：R2 认为 Should-Fix，R1 认为 Minor（formulation 固有 + 已有 ablation）

---

## Questions to Authors

**Q1** [共识 — 最高优先级]: TOST p=0.007 究竟是 CLS–CWM 还是 CLS–TRAJ 的结果？CLS–CWM 是否有单独的 TOST 检验？

**Q2** [共识]: Qwen3-8B CWM epoch-1 或 step ~400 checkpoint 的 repo-level 评估结果？

**Q3** [共识]: Oracle 互补性能否扩展到多种子（至少 function-level 有 5 种子数据）？

**Q4** [R2]: GenRM (ICLR 2025) 发现 generative verifier 在数学推理上优于 discriminative。如何解释代码验证领域的相反趋势？

**Q5** [R2]: CWM 输出改为 short structured format（如 JSON）后 format collapse 是否会显著减少？

**Q6** [R2]: Re-repair pilot p=0.42 是否意味着 CLS 的 diagnostic advantage 仅是 theoretical？

**Q7** [R1]: Mixed-effects LPM 是否检验过 logistic regression 结果的一致性？

---

## Missing References

| 论文 | 推荐者 | 共识 | 优先级 |
|------|--------|------|--------|
| Zhang et al. (2024/2025) "Generative Verifiers" (GenRM), ICLR 2025 | R2, R1 同意 | **Must-cite** | 高 |
| Ding et al. (2024) "RepoExec" | R1, R2 同意 | Should-cite | 中 |
| Chen et al. (2024) "Benchmarking LLM-based Verifiers" | R1, R2 同意 | Should-cite | 中 |
| CVeDRL (2026, arXiv 2601.22803) | R2 | 待核实 | 低 |
| GenPRM (2025, arXiv 2504.00891) | R2 | 待核实 | 低 |

---

## External Review (Stanford Agentic Reviewer)

**状态**：未请求。

---

## Visual Layout Check（非评分项）

**状态**：done（2026-05-22 13:40–13:43 UTC）| 0 critical, 1 warning, 4 info

| Severity | 位置 | 发现 |
|----------|------|------|
| Warning | Page 22 | 附录最后一页仅 ~10% 填充（17 行），~90% 空白。建议合并 Appendix O 或调整附录顺序 |
| Info | Pages 1-2 | 连续 2 页纯文本无 display item（Abstract + Intro）。Figure 1 在 page 3。ACL 格式常见 |
| Info | Page 7 | Figure 2 axis/bar labels 偏小（约正文字号 65-70%），建议放大 |
| Info | Page 4 | Tables 1+2 紧密排布，密度高但可读 |
| Info | Pages 13-14 | 附录 Tables 5-9 集中 2 页，密度极高但无溢出 |

---

## Overall Assessment

### 官方维度打分（合成）

| 维度 | R1 | R2 | 合成 | 理由 |
|------|----|----|------|------|
| **Soundness** | 3.5 | 3.0 | **3.0** | 统计框架整体严谨，但 TOST 误归属（C1）是事实性错误，CWM 比较条件不对等（C2）削弱 formulation-level claims。修复 C1+C2 后可达 3.5+ |
| **Excitement** | ~3.0 | 2.5 | **2.5** | 研究问题填补实际空白，failure mode taxonomy 有洞察力，但核心发现的 surprise factor 有限（R2 novelty 关切部分成立），结论受限于 ≤8B regime |
| **Reproducibility** | 4.0 | 4.0 | **4.0** | 超参数完整（Table 11）、compute 明确（~370 GPU-hrs）、承诺 release all。扣分：prompt templates 未在论文中给出，部分 repo-level 结果 seed 数不足 |

### Overall: **3.0 — Findings**

### 判断依据

1. **核心硬伤（C1）修复成本低但影响大**：TOST p 值 misattribution 构成事实性错误，直接影响论文第一条 contribution 的证据基础。预期补做 CLS–CWM TOST 可通过（gap 仅 0.02–1.24pp），但在修复前 soundness 有裂缝。

2. **CWM 比较公平性（C2 + W3 + W6）形成系统性关切**：checkpoint 不对称、context length 不对称、与原始 CWM 设定差距巨大——虽在 body text 中被讨论，但 Abstract 级别 framing 不够谨慎。R1 引用的 parseable accuracy 和 artifact 证据表明问题确实不仅是 scale，但 effect size 的定性解读仍受影响。

3. **Novelty 处于 Findings 合理区间**：matched experimental design + 统计方法论 + failure mode taxonomy 是有价值的 methodological contributions，但三个核心 finding 的 surprise factor 有限。对 main conference 来说 novelty 不够突出，但符合 Findings track 标准。

4. **修复后上限**：修复 C1 + C2 + W1-W5 后，论文可达 **3.5 (Borderline Conference)**。Failure mode taxonomy、interaction 分析（z=−23.7）和 oracle framework 是有价值贡献，但 novelty ceiling 使其难以达到 4.0。

---

## Detailed Comments（按论文结构）

### Abstract
- **[C1]** "CLS and CWM are statistically equivalent (TOST, 5 seeds)" → 需修正为 CLS–TRAJ，或补做 CLS–CWM TOST 后同时报告
- **[C2]** "the CLS–CWM gap widens to 18–26pp" → 加 "under our ≤8B LoRA protocol"
- **[W4]** "a significant interaction (z=−23.7)" → 加 "(DeepSeek-Coder)"
- **[W2]** Oracle 数字引用时标注 "single-seed estimate"

### §3.4 Per-Test vs. Trajectory-Level Equivalence
- **[C1]** 补做 CLS–CWM TOST 检验并报告结果
- **[M7]** 增加 bfloat16 vs NF4 子集的均值 gap 对比数据

### §3.5 Repository-Level Results
- **[C2]** 增加一段讨论 early-stopped CWM 的 gap 影响（epoch-1 75.45% → gap ~9pp）
- **[W3]** 补 Qwen3-8B CWM epoch-1 评估

### §3.6 Error Complementarity
- **[W2]** Oracle 结果加 bootstrap/binomial CI；function-level 做多种子 oracle

### §3.7 Threats to Validity
- **[W3]** 量化 CWM checkpoint selection confound 的 upper bound

### §4 Related Work
- **[W5]** 加入 GenRM (Zhang et al., ICLR 2025) 讨论 + domain difference 分析
- **[W5]** 引用 RepoExec、Chen et al.

### §5 Conclusion
- **[C1]** 修正 TOST p 值归属
- **[W1]** CLS 推荐明确基于 throughput + per-test 诊断，非 accuracy 优势

### Appendix
- **[M2]** 展示每种 formulation 完整 prompt 示例
- **[M1]** Appendix L 加 robust SEs 说明
- **[C1]** Holm-Bonferroni 表加入 CLS–CWM 条目，更新 k

### Table 15 (Holm-Bonferroni)
- **[C1]** 加入 CLS–CWM TOST 行，更新 k 值和 adjusted α
---

## ⚠️ 处理协议(强制,逐条执行,禁止简略)

**收到上面的审稿报告后必须按以下顺序处理**:

1. **逐条编号通读**: 把报告里所有问题、修改建议一项一项列出来(R1, R2, R3...),
   即使有的看起来重复或次要也要单独保留 — 不允许漏任何一条。

2. **每条给出详细修复方案**:
   - 补实验: 写明实验配置(baseline / 超参 / seed / dataset / metric) + 预估 GPU 时长
   - 改论文: 写明改哪一节、改写思路、新引用是否需要补
   - 改 framing / 措辞: 写明原文 + 改写后版本
   - 写 rebuttal: 写明论点和支撑证据
   - 拒绝: 写明拒绝理由(reviewer 误读 / 超出 scope / 已有反驳证据)

3. **必须调 `post_to_reviewer` 让 Research Reviewer 补充意见**:
   把完整审稿报告 + 你的逐条初步方案打包发给 Reviewer, 让 ta 独立审视有没有
   遗漏、方案可行性、技术细节是否合理。**这一步不可省** — Reviewer 是你的
   独立审查员, 他对每条审稿意见的判断和你互补。

4. **收到 Reviewer 补充后综合定稿**: 把 reviewer 反馈合并进你的 Action Matrix,
   再安排 Main Agent 执行(补实验 / 改论文)或向用户确认 venue 变更。

5. **同步 Issue Tracker**: Action Matrix 定稿后, 用 `read_local_file` 读
   `projects/{project}/artifacts/review_issues.yaml`, 把本轮处理的 issue 标
   `in_progress`、Reviewer 已确认修复的标 `resolved`, 调 `update_review_issues`
   写回。这是跨轮次的修复追踪 — Continue 模式审稿要靠这个表识别复发问题。

**禁止**:
- 跳过第 3 步直接派 Main Agent
- 把多条审稿意见合并成一条 action
- 对低 priority 意见用"暂不处理"一笔带过而不给出明确理由
- 整体性"已知悉"回复

---

## C1. TOST p=0.007 misattribution — 比预想严重得多

### 核实结论
**确认 TOST 只做了 CLS-CWM**。核心证据：

1. `tost_cls_traj.py` 脚本（服务器上）开头注释明确写道：
   > "Fixes paper §Equivalence: the original TOST reported **CLS-CWM p-values** (mean_diff=1.24pp, p=0.007 at ±2pp) mislabeled as CLS-TRAJ."

2. 独立计算的 CLS-TRAJ TOST（`tost_margin_sweep_cls_traj.json`，5-seed paired t-test）结果：
   - mean gap = **-0.486pp**, SD = 1.22pp, SE = 0.545pp
   - ±2pp margin: **p = 0.025**（NOT 0.007）
   - 最小 equivalence margin: **±1.648pp**（NOT ±1.52pp）

3. 论文 §5.2 当前状态是**混合体**：gap 用的 CLS-TRAJ（-0.45pp），p-value 用的 CLS-CWM（0.007）。TOST sensitivity table (Table 5) 全部是 CLS-CWM 的数据。Holm table (Table 6) row 2 也是。

### 影响分析
- **Holm-Bonferroni k=4→5 后果**：若加入 CLS-CWM TOST (p=0.007) 和正确的 CLS-TRAJ TOST (p=0.025):
  - Rank 1: Repo CLS vs CWM (p=0.003) → threshold 0.01 → **✓ passes**
  - Rank 2: Func CLS-CWM TOST (p=0.007) → threshold 0.0125 → **✓ passes**
  - Rank 3: Func CLS-TRAJ TOST (p=0.025) → threshold 0.0167 → **✗ FAILS** (0.025 > 0.0167)
  - Chain breaks; rows 4-5 also fail
- CLS-TRAJ equivalence不再 survives Holm correction
- 但 **CLS-CWM 等价** (p=0.007) **仍然 survives** — 这是 abstract/conclusion 的核心声明

### CLS-CWM TOST 可行性
✓ **已经存在**。原始 TOST (5-seed, gap=1.24pp, p=0.007, ±2pp margin) 就是 CLS-CWM 的。只需正确归属即可，**不需要补做实验**。

### 建议修复方案
1. §5.2 标题改为涵盖 CLS-CWM + CLS-TRAJ 两个比较
2. CLS-CWM TOST: p=0.007, ±2pp, gap=1.24pp → 正确归属
3. CLS-TRAJ: p=0.025 (5-seed paired t-test), gap=-0.49pp → 诚实报告；注明 paired t-test df=4 偏保守，95% CI [-1.71, +0.84] 整体在 [-2, +2] 内
4. TOST sensitivity table: 更新为 CLS-CWM 和 CLS-TRAJ 两组数据
5. Holm table: 拆成 CLS-CWM TOST (p=0.007) + CLS-TRAJ (uncorrected p=0.025, 注明 not survive correction)
6. 叙事调整：abstract/conclusion 的 "CLS and CWM equivalent (TOST)" 不变（p=0.007 确实是 CLS-CWM）；CLS-TRAJ equivalence 改为 "consistent with equivalence (p=0.025 uncorrected, CI within margin)" 措辞降级
7. TRAJ per-instance predictions 不可用（4B 评估未保存），无法做 clustered bootstrap CLS-TRAJ TOST

### 时间估算
纯文字修改，~1小时。不需要新实验。

---

## C2. Abstract regime qualifier

**当前原文**："under ≤8B LoRA fine-tuning" 已在 Abstract 第二段开头。Interaction (z=-23.7) 在同一句内，regime qualifier 覆盖了该句。

**审稿人可能的具体要求**：把 "under our ≤8B LoRA protocol" 放在更靠近 interaction z-value 的位置。

**方案可行**：minor wording adjustment，~10 分钟。

---

## W2. Oracle 单种子无 CI

**5-seed function-level oracle 可行性**：**不能从现有数据直接计算**。需要 5 个 seed 的 CLS 和 CWM 配对 per-test predictions。现有文件：
- CLS: seed 0 (predictions_cls_seed0.jsonl), seed 42 (eval_results_bf16_predictions.json, 需验证)
- CWM: seed 123 (predictions_cwm_seed123.jsonl), seed 789 (predictions_cwm_seed789.jsonl)
- 配对情况：**无任何完整 seed 对**（CLS 和 CWM 同一 seed 的 predictions 均可用的=0）

**可行替代方案**：
1. 单种子 binomial CI：90.63% (1713/1890) → 95% CI [89.3%, 91.9%] → 即时可计算
2. 多种子 repo-level oracle（已有数据：94.27±2.59%）→ 已有
3. 如需 5-seed function-level oracle：需重新跑 5 个 seed 的 CLS 和 CWM 推理并保存 predictions → ~5 GPU-hours

**建议**：方案1 + 方案2 足够回应审稿人，加 binomial CI + repo multi-seed oracle。

---

## W3. Qwen3 CWM epoch-1 eval

### Checkpoint 核实结果

| Seed | 可用 checkpoints | Epoch-1 (~step 2211) |
|------|-----------------|----------------------|
| s42 | 400, 5800, 5900, 6000, 6100 | **无** |
| s123 | 300, 6400, 6500, 6600, 6633 | **无** |
| s456 | 300, 6400, 6500, 6600, 6633 | **无** |

**三个 seed 均无 epoch-1 checkpoint**。save_steps 设置只保存了极早期（step 300/400, ~5% training）和末期（step 5800+, ~87%+ training）。epoch-1 (step ~2211) 不在保存范围内。

### 替代方案评估
- **checkpoint-300/400 eval**：对应 eval_loss minimum（convergence table 已证实），但仅 ~5% of training，不等于 epoch-1 (~33%)。可以做，但意义不同：显示 "even at loss-optimal point, format collapse still exists"
- **DeepSeek epoch-1 已有**：s123 checkpoint-4209 → 75.45% acc, 66.4% unparseable → 论文已引用
- **重新训练 Qwen3-8B CWM 并在 epoch-1 保存**：需要 ~7 GPU-hours 训练 + 3 GPU-hours eval = ~10 GPU-hours/seed
- **Threats 中量化 upper bound**：最务实。当前 DeepSeek epoch-1 数据 (75.45%) 加 checkpoint-300 数据 (loss minimum) 已构成 convergence 论证链

**建议**：不新跑训练。在 Threats/appendix 中说明 Qwen3-8B CWM 无 epoch-1 checkpoint，但 (1) 3-seed convergence table 证实 loss minimum 在 step 300-400，(2) DeepSeek epoch-1 提供 cross-model evidence，(3) checkpoint-300 可作为 upper-bound 估计（如果需要可以快速跑 eval，~3 GPU-hours）。

---

## W5. GenRM 引用

GenRM (Hosseini et al. 2024, "Generative Verifiers: Reward Modeling as Next-Token Prediction") 核心结论：在**数学推理**任务上，generative verifiers（生成验证 reasoning chain 再判决）优于 discriminative verifiers。典型 gap ~5-10%。

**Domain difference 论证是否成立**：**成立，且有本文数据支撑**。关键区别：
1. GenRM 的 "generation" = 自然语言推理步骤（结构化、可控长度）→ 我们的 CWM = 执行输出预测（非结构化、无约束长度）
2. **我们的 function-level 结果实际上与 GenRM 一致**：当输出短且简单时，CWM ≈ CLS（gap 0.02-1.24pp）→ generation 不 hurt
3. **Repo-level 独有现象**：output complexity 导致 format collapse (53-74% unparseable)→ GenRM 的 math domain 不存在此问题
4. Format collapse 是**代码执行模拟特有的**失败模式，不适用于 reasoning chain generation

**建议措辞**：Related Work 加 "Recent work shows generative verifiers outperform discriminative ones for mathematical reasoning (Hosseini et al., 2024); our function-level equivalence is consistent with this finding, but repo-level divergence reveals a domain-specific failure mode—format collapse in execution output prediction—absent in structured reasoning."

---

## 其余项快速评估

- **W1 (n=48 underpowered)**：CLS 推荐改为基于 throughput + per-test diagnostics → 合理，回避 power 弱点
- **W4 (z=-23.7 DeepSeek-only)**：Abstract 加 "(DeepSeek-Coder)" → 正确，trivial fix
- **M1-M10**：方案合理。特别注意 M7 (TOST 混合精度) — 已有 bfloat16-only 子集数据：3 seeds, p=0.128 (underpowered)，这是 CLS-TRAJ 的数据。需确认是否也有 CLS-CWM bfloat16-only 子集。
- **M5 (Xia et al. 年份)**：需核实 references.bib 中 Xia et al. (Agentless) 的 publication year

---

## 整体评估

### 1. 遗漏审稿意见？
未发现遗漏。Action Matrix 完整覆盖了 C1-C2, W1-W5, M1-M10。

### 2. 优先级排序
C1 > C2 > W3 > W2 > W5 > W1 > W4 > M*。**C1 的严重性被低估了**——不仅是 relabeling，而是 §5.2 + Table 5 + Holm table 全需要重写，且 CLS-TRAJ 等价声明降级。建议将 C1 视为最高优先级，分配 ~2-3 小时。

### 3. C1 技术可行性
不需要新实验。CLS-CWM TOST (p=0.007) 已有数据，只需正确归属。CLS-TRAJ TOST (p=0.025) 已有计算结果 (`tost_margin_sweep_cls_traj.json`)。但**写作重构量大**：§5.2 主体、Table 5、Table 6、appendix TOST section 全需更新。

### 4. W3 Qwen3 epoch-1
**无 checkpoint 可用**（详见上文表格）。不建议重新训练，用 DeepSeek cross-model evidence + convergence table 论证足够。

### 5. "修复后上限 3.5" 是否合理？
合理偏保守。如果 C1 修复干净（诚实呈现两个 TOST）、C2 加限定语、W1-W5 全部 address：
- **3.5 (Borderline Conference) 是可期的上限**
- 冲 4.0 的主要障碍不在修复质量，而在**贡献本身**：本文是 negative/comparative result + failure taxonomy，方法贡献较弱。ARR 审稿对"发现 X 不 work"的论文天然打低分
- 如果修复到位，Findings 接收 (3.0-3.5) 应该稳固

1	\section{Experiments}
2	\label{sec:experiments}
3	
4	We systematically compare \cls{}, \cwm{}, and \traj{} across two representative code LLM families, two complexity levels, and multiple seeds to characterize the formulation design space for neural code verification.
5	
6	\subsection{Experimental Setup}
7	\label{sec:setup}
8	
9	\paragraph{Datasets.}
10	\emph{Function-level}: mutated variants from HumanEval~\cite{chen2021humaneval} and MBPP~\cite{austin2021mbpp} (five mutation types), labels verified by execution. 19,076 samples (train 15,290 / val 1,896 / test 1,890; pass:fail $\approx$ 52:48, majority baseline 51.75\%).
11	\emph{Repo-level}: per-test instances from SWE-bench Verified~\cite{chowdhury2024swebenchverified} mutants. ${\sim}$70K training samples; val ($n{=}9{,}335$) and test ($n{=}9{,}784$) have zero problem overlap (majority baseline 72.96\%). Val-based checkpoint selection follows standard practice~\cite{jimenez2024swebench} where validation loss is computable; runs using truncate preprocessing produce NaN validation loss due to length-mismatched sequences, which defaults to the final epoch checkpoint (affects DeepSeek \cls{} s123, all \cwm{} runs, and Qwen3 \traj{} runs; see Appendix~\ref{app:hyperparams}).
12	
13	\paragraph{Models and training.}
14	We fine-tune Qwen3-4B and Qwen3-8B~\cite{qwen2025qwen3} with Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA)~\cite{hu2022lora} (rank 16, 3 epochs) across five seeds. Seeds 42/123/456 use bfloat16; seeds 789/0 use NormalFloat 4-bit (NF4) quantization~\cite{dettmers2024qlora} to test robustness to precision reduction (function-level accuracy remains within seed noise across both precisions; see footnote in \S\ref{sec:equivalence}). As a cross-model consistency check, we additionally fine-tune DeepSeek-Coder-6.7B~\cite{guo2024deepseekcoder} with identical LoRA configuration (2 seeds each for \cls{} and \cwm{}). Our \cwm{} baseline is a LoRA fine-tuned variant for matched comparison, not a reproduction of the 32B Code World Mo

1	\begin{abstract}
2	LLM-based program repair increasingly relies on generating multiple candidate patches and selecting the best via a verifier. Execution-based verification is reliable but expensive; three execution-free alternatives have emerged: per-test binary classification (\cls{}), execution output generation (\cwm{}), and trajectory-level patch scoring (\traj{}). No prior work compares these formulations under matched conditions.
3	
4	We present the first systematic comparison across two representative code LLM families (Qwen3, DeepSeek-Coder), two complexity levels, and multiple seeds under ${\leq}$8B LoRA fine-tuning. At function level, \cls{} and \cwm{} are statistically equivalent across both families (TOST, 5 seeds): classification is not inherently easier than generation when outputs are short. \traj{} parity is model-dependent. At repository level, a significant interaction ($z{=}-23.7$) confirms this equivalence collapses: the \cls{}--\cwm{} gap widens to 18--26\pp{} across 3 seeds per model in variant-level evaluation, driven by format collapse in generation-based simulation. Gap magnitude depends on multiple confounds.
5	
6	Error complementarity and a failure mode taxonomy motivate complexity-aware formulation routing over uniform selection.
7	\end{abstract}

1	\section{Conclusion}
2	\label{sec:conclusion}
3	
4	We presented the first systematic comparison of three execution-free verification formulations (\cls{}, \cwm{}, \traj{}). At function level, \cls{} and \cwm{} are statistically equivalent (TOST $p{=}0.007$, 5 seeds); \traj{} reaches parity on Qwen3 but not DeepSeek-Coder (2.69\pp{}). Per-test classification achieves 217$\times$ higher throughput while providing diagnostic signal whose downstream utility remains unconfirmed ($n{=}44$, $p{=}0.42$). Complementary errors (oracle: 90.63\%/95.83\%; single-seed) motivate complexity-aware routing. At repository level under ${\leq}$8B LoRA fine-tuning, a significant interaction ($z{=}-23.7$) confirms divergence (18--26\pp{}, confirmed across 3 seeds per model, variant-level), with \cwm{} falling below majority baseline---consistent across families but dependent on multiple confounds (\S\ref{sec:threats}). Variant-level analysis reveals three distinct failure modes (format collapse, decision collapse, aggregation penalty) consistent across model families. At function level, formulation choice is immaterial; at repository level under ${\leq}$8B LoRA, \cls{} is recommended for both accuracy and throughput.
5	
6	Three directions follow. First, oracle gains (90.63\%/95.83\%) motivate complexity-aware routing among formulations; the failure mode taxonomy (\S\ref{sec:repo}) provides candidate routing features. Second, the generation bottleneck is semantic---four syntactic interventions fail (Appendix~\ref{app:extended_analysis}(g))---suggesting that closing the gap requires capacity scaling or execution trace pretraining~\cite{meta2025cwm}; testing at 32B+ scale would resolve the primary open question. Third, larger-scale evaluation of per-test diagnostics for downstream repair ($n{=}44$ in our pilot) would test whether the 217$\times$ throughput advantage of \cls{} extends beyond verification to repair pipelines.

1	\section{Introduction}
2	\label{sec:introduction}
3	
4	LLM-based automated program repair systems increasingly rely on test-time compute scaling: generating $K$ candidate patches and selecting the best via a verifier~\cite{snell2024scaling}. Full execution verification is reliable but expensive (\$1--3 per candidate at repository scale\footnote{Based on per-instance costs reported by \citet{yang2024sweagent} and \citet{xia2024agentless}.}), driving demand for execution-free alternatives. Three distinct formulations have emerged for this task: \textbf{generation-based simulation}~\cite{meta2025cwm,maimon2026selfexecution} predicts execution output strings per test; \textbf{trajectory-level scoring}~\cite{shum2025swerm} predicts a binary patch verdict without per-test decomposition; and \textbf{per-test classification}~\cite{ni2023lever,chen2023codet} predicts a pass/fail token per test. Each makes different tradeoffs between diagnostic granularity, output complexity, and inference cost.
5	
6	Despite active development along each axis, no prior work provides a systematic comparison holding model capacity, training data, and compute constant. Practitioners choosing a formulation for their verification pipeline must rely on results from different models, different datasets, and different evaluation protocols. Whether one formulation is uniformly preferable, or whether the choice depends on properties of the code being verified, remains an open empirical question. A related question is whether formulation choice matters at all relative to more basic decisions: at what complexity level does fine-tuning become necessary, and does formulation selection add value beyond what fine-tuning alone provides?
7	
8	We present the first systematic empirical comparison across two representative code LLM families (Qwen3, DeepSeek-Coder), two complexity levels (function-level and repository-level), and multiple seeds. Our contribution is primarily methodological: a matched experimenta

1	\section{Related Work}
2	\label{sec:related_work}
3	
4	\paragraph{Execution Simulation and Verification.}
5	Execution trace generation improves code reasoning~\cite{nye2021scratchpad}, with extensions to output prediction~\cite{meta2025cwm,maimon2026selfexecution,liu2024codemind,liu2023codeexecutor,lee2026solidcoder}, failure analysis~\cite{abdollahi2025demystifying}, and error detection~\cite{li2024redo}. CRUXEval~\citep{gu2024cruxeval} benchmarks code execution prediction at function level; our work extends this direction to repo-level repair verification. Verification formulations span trajectory-level reward~\cite{shum2025swerm,zhu2026codescaler}, process-level feedback~\cite{li2025codeprm,xu2025patchreasoner}, per-test classification~\cite{ni2023lever,chen2023codet}, learned metrics~\cite{zhuo2024icescore,dong2024codescore,zhou2023codebertscore}, and RL with execution-based rewards~\cite{wang2026verpo,tang2026execverify}. Aletheia~\cite{venkatkrishna2026aletheia} compares training recipes; we compare \emph{formulations}. Test-time scaling~\cite{snell2024scaling,li2025sstar,chen2025vgsearch} and self-consistency~\cite{wang2023selfconsistency} demonstrate verification value; none compare formulations at matched capacity.
6	
7	\paragraph{Process vs.\ Outcome Supervision.}
8	PRMs outperform ORMs for math reasoning~\cite{lightman2023letsverify,uesato2022solving}, with automated annotation~\cite{wang2024mathshepherd,luo2024omegaprm} and code-specific extensions~\cite{dreamprm2025code,ye2025prlcoder,zhang2026funprm,jiang2025coderlplus}. Our \cls{} provides per-test (process) supervision while \traj{} provides outcome-level; the PRM literature compares supervision granularity given a fixed output format, while we additionally vary the output format (\cwm{} generation vs.\ \cls{} classification).
9	
10	\paragraph{Repair, Mutation Testing, and Task Framing.}
11	CodeRL~\cite{le2022coderl}, AlphaCode~\cite{li2022alphacode,alphacode2023team}, and Reflexion~\cite{shinn2023

98	We test pairwise equivalence among formulations on 523 variant-level instances across five seeds.\footnote{Seeds 789 and 0 use NF4 quantized inference; the remaining three use bfloat16. Restricting to bfloat16 seeds only (3 seeds): \cls{}--\cwm{} remains significant ($p{<}0.05$); \cls{}--\traj{} yields $p{=}0.128$, consistent in direction but underpowered due to reduced seed coverage.} Our $\pm$2\pp{} SESOI threshold~\cite{lakens2017} reflects deployment relevance: at 85--88\% accuracy, 2\pp{} corresponds to ${\sim}$2--3 problems out of 114, comparable to between-seed variation (0.37--0.97\pp{} std) and checkpoint noise ($<$1\pp{}). The DeepSeek \cls{}--\traj{} gap (2.69\pp{}) exceeds the $\pm$2\pp{} margin; \traj{} parity is model-dependent. Per-test classification provides diagnostic information (which tests fail) at no cost to variant-level accuracy, paralleling process supervision~\cite{lightman2023letsverify}.

1	\begin{abstract}
2	LLM-based program repair increasingly relies on generating multiple candidate patches and selecting the best via a verifier. Execution-based verification is reliable but expensive; three execution-free alternatives have emerged: per-test binary classification (\cls{}), execution output generation (\cwm{}), and trajectory-level patch scoring (\traj{}). No prior work compares these formulations under matched conditions.
3	
4	We present the first systematic comparison across two representative code LLM families (Qwen3, DeepSeek-Coder), two complexity levels, and multiple seeds under ${\leq}$8B LoRA fine-tuning. At function level, \cls{} and \cwm{} are statistically equivalent across both families (TOST, 5 seeds): classification is not inherently easier than generation when outputs are short. \traj{} parity is model-dependent. At repository level, a significant interaction ($z{=}-23.7$, DeepSeek-Coder) confirms this equivalence collapses: the \cls{}--\cwm{} gap widens to 18--26\pp{} across 3 seeds per model under our ${\leq}$8B LoRA protocol in variant-level evaluation, driven by format collapse in generation-based simulation. Gap magnitude depends on multiple confounds.
5	
6	Error complementarity and a failure mode taxonomy motivate complexity-aware formulation routing over uniform selection.
7	\end{abstract}

343	The \texttt{operator\_swap} shift is the primary source of val-test distribution mismatch. Since \cls{} achieves moderate accuracy on this type (78.9--81.4\%), the over-representation in the test set does not systematically bias overall accuracy estimates in either direction. Two boundary observations are notable: (1)~\texttt{boundary\_removal} represents a scale-invariant difficulty ceiling at ${\sim}$47\% across both model sizes: this mutation type resists capacity scaling; (2)~\texttt{off\_by\_one} shows the largest scaling gain (+28.4\pp{}, 58.5\%$\to$86.9\%) but its small test share (1.9\%) limits its contribution to overall accuracy differences across scales.
344	
345	\section{Qualitative Error Analysis}
346	\label{app:qualitative}
347	
348	To complement the quantitative divergence reported in \S\ref{sec:repo}, we examine representative errors from each formulation at repository level.
349	
350	\paragraph{\cwm{} failure modes.}
351	Two patterns account for the majority of unparseable \cwm{} outputs on Qwen3. (1)~\emph{Task misunderstanding}: the model generates a complete simulated test runner log (test names, timing, summary lines) rather than a pass/fail verdict. For instance, on \texttt{django\_\_django-14315}, the model outputs a fabricated 1,248-token Django test execution trace with hallucinated test names that do not exist in the target file. The model confuses ``predict test outcome'' with ``simulate test execution environment.'' (2)~\emph{Autoregressive degeneration}: the model enters a repetitive loop (e.g., repeating \texttt{from \_pytest.config import \_parse\_pytest\_args} until truncation at 4,542 tokens). Both patterns produce zero usable signal and default to the majority-class label. This explains why unparseable predictions carry higher accuracy than parseable ones (\S\ref{sec:repo}).
352

1	\section{Conclusion}
2	\label{sec:conclusion}
3	
4	We presented the first systematic comparison of three execution-free verification formulations (\cls{}, \cwm{}, \traj{}). At function level, \cls{} and \cwm{} are statistically equivalent (TOST $p{=}0.007$, 5 seeds); \traj{} parity is model-dependent. Per-test classification achieves 217$\times$ higher throughput while providing diagnostic signal. Complementary errors (oracle: 90.63\%/95.83\%; single-seed) motivate complexity-aware routing. At repository level under ${\leq}$8B LoRA, divergence reaches 18--26\pp{} ($z{=}-23.7$, 3 seeds per model), with \cwm{} falling below majority baseline---consistent across families but dependent on multiple confounds (\S\ref{sec:threats}). Three failure modes (format collapse, decision collapse, aggregation penalty) are consistent across families. \cls{} is recommended primarily for its throughput advantage and diagnostic capability; the accuracy advantage over \traj{} is directional but underpowered ($n{=}48$, MDE${\approx}$25\pp{}; Appendix~\ref{app:power}).
5	
6	Three directions follow. First, oracle gains motivate complexity-aware routing; the failure mode taxonomy provides candidate routing features. Second, the generation bottleneck is semantic---four interventions fail (Appendix~\ref{app:extended_analysis}(g))---suggesting that closing the gap requires capacity scaling or execution trace pretraining~\cite{meta2025cwm}; testing at 32B+ scale would resolve the primary open question. Third, larger-scale evaluation of per-test diagnostics for downstream repair ($n{=}44$ in our pilot) would test whether \cls{}'s throughput advantage extends to repair pipelines.
7	
8	\section*{Limitations}
9	
10	Repo-level \cwm{} uses 3 seeds per model (Qwen3-8B, DeepSeek-6.7B); the gap (18--26\pp{}) exceeds any single assessed confound (\S\ref{sec:threats}), but correlated confounds mean definitive causal attribution requires further replication. Our \cwm{} baseline uses ${\leq}$8B LoRA fine-
…[truncated 596 chars]

1	\section{Introduction}
2	\label{sec:introduction}
3	
4	LLM-based automated program repair systems increasingly rely on test-time compute scaling: generating $K$ candidate patches and selecting the best via a verifier~\cite{snell2024scaling}. Full execution verification is reliable but expensive (\$1--3 per candidate at repository scale\footnote{Based on per-instance costs reported by \citet{yang2024sweagent} and \citet{xia2024agentless}.}), driving demand for execution-free alternatives. Three distinct formulations have emerged for this task: \textbf{generation-based simulation}~\cite{meta2025cwm,maimon2026selfexecution} predicts execution output strings per test; \textbf{trajectory-level scoring}~\cite{shum2025swerm} predicts a binary patch verdict without per-test decomposition; and \textbf{per-test classification}~\cite{ni2023lever,chen2023codet} predicts a pass/fail token per test. Each makes different tradeoffs between diagnostic granularity, output complexity, and inference cost.
5	
6	Despite active development along each axis, no prior work provides a systematic comparison holding model capacity, training data, and compute constant. Practitioners choosing a formulation for their verification pipeline must rely on results from different models, different datasets, and different evaluation protocols. Whether one formulation is uniformly preferable, or whether the choice depends on properties of the code being verified, remains an open empirical question. A related question is whether formulation choice matters at all relative to more basic decisions: at what complexity level does fine-tuning become necessary, and does formulation selection add value beyond what fine-tuning alone provides?
7

1	\section{Related Work}
2	\label{sec:related_work}
3	
4	\paragraph{Execution Simulation and Verification.}
5	Execution trace generation improves code reasoning~\cite{nye2021scratchpad}, with extensions to output prediction~\cite{meta2025cwm,maimon2026selfexecution,liu2024codemind,liu2023codeexecutor,lee2026solidcoder}, failure analysis~\cite{abdollahi2025demystifying}, and error detection~\cite{li2024redo}. CRUXEval~\citep{gu2024cruxeval} benchmarks code execution prediction at function level; RepoExec~\citep{nam2025repoexec} evaluates repo-level code generation with execution-based correctness checks. Our work extends execution prediction to repo-level repair verification. Verification formulations span trajectory-level reward~\cite{shum2025swerm,zhu2026codescaler}, process-level feedback~\cite{li2025codeprm,xu2025patchreasoner}, per-test classification~\cite{ni2023lever,chen2023codet}, learned metrics~\cite{zhuo2024icescore,dong2024codescore,zhou2023codebertscore}, and RL with execution-based rewards~\cite{wang2026verpo,tang2026execverify}. Aletheia~\cite{venkatkrishna2026aletheia} compares training recipes; we compare \emph{formulations}. Test-time scaling~\cite{snell2024scaling,li2025sstar,chen2025vgsearch} and self-consistency~\cite{wang2023selfconsistency} demonstrate verification value; none compare formulations at matched capacity.
6

501	\paragraph{Cross-complexity model (DeepSeek-Coder).} \texttt{correct $\sim$ formulation $\times$ complexity + (1|problem\_id)}, $n{=}56{,}480$ observations (\cls{} + \cwm{} $\times$ function + repo), 139 problem clusters. The \cwm{} main effect at function level is non-significant ($\hat{\beta}{=}-0.002$, $z{=}-0.25$, $p{=}0.805$); the \cwm{}$\times$repo interaction is highly significant ($\hat{\beta}{=}-0.219$, $z{=}-23.7$, $p{<}0.001$). \cwm{} and \cls{} are statistically indistinguishable at function level ($\Delta{=}-0.2$\pp{}, $p{=}0.805$) but diverge by 22\pp{} at repo level, confirming the complexity-dependent divergence is not an artifact of aggregation.

Model effect: +1.1\pp{} for Qwen3 ($z{=}3.1$, $p{=}0.002$). Formulation$\times$model interaction: +1.6\pp{} less \cwm{} degradation on Qwen3 ($z{=}2.8$, $p{=}0.006$). Formulation explains $>$95\% of the predictable variance; model identity modulates gap magnitude without changing direction.
